string `json:\"full_name\"`\n\tDescription string `json:\"description\"`\n\tLanguage    string `json:\"language,omitempty\"`\n\tURL         string `json:\"url\"`\n\tPushedAt    string `json:\"pushed_at\"`\n}\n\ntype SuggestionsSourceStore interface {\n\tListAllSourceRepos(ctx context.Context) ([]models.SourceRepo, error)\n}\n\ntype suggestionsCache struct {\n\tdata      json.RawMessage\n\tfetchedAt time.Time\n}\n\ntype SuggestionsHandler struct {\n\tclient    *http.Client\n\tstore     SuggestionsSourceStore\n\ttoken     string\n\tgithubURL string\n\tmu        sync.Mutex\n\tcache     map[string]suggestionsCache\n}\n\nfunc NewSuggestionsHandler(client *http.Client, store SuggestionsSourceStore, token, githubURL string) *SuggestionsHandler {\n\tif token == \"\" {\n\t\ttoken = os.Getenv(\"GITHUB_TOKEN\")\n\t}\n\tif githubURL == \"\" {\n\t\tgithubURL = \"https://api.github.com\"\n\t}\n\treturn &SuggestionsHandler{\n\t\tclient:    client,\n\t\tstore:     store,\n\t\ttoken:     token,\n\t\tgithubURL: githubURL,\n\t\tcache:     make(map[string]suggestionsCache),\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (h *SuggestionsHandler) getCached(key string) (json.RawMessage, bool) {\n\th.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer h.mu.Unlock()\n\tc, ok := h.cache[key]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn nil, false\n\t}\n\tif time.Since(c.fetchedAt) > suggestionsCacheTTL {\n\t\tdelete(h.cache, key)\n\t\treturn nil, false\n\t}\n\treturn c.data, true\n}\n\nfunc (h *SuggestionsHandler) setCache(key string, data json.RawMessage) {\n\th.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer h.mu.Unlock()\n\th.cache[key] = suggestionsCache{data: data, fetchedAt: time.Now()}\n}\n\nfunc (h *SuggestionsHandler) githubGet(ctx context.Context, path string) (json.RawMessage, error) {\n\turl := h.githubURL + path\n\treq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, \"GET\", url, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tif h.token != \"\" {\n\t\treq.Header.Set(\"Authorization\", \"Bearer \"+h.token)\n\t}\n\treq.Header.Set(\"Accept\", \"application/vnd.github+json\")\n\n\tresp, err := h.client.Do(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer resp.Body.Close()\n\n\tif resp.StatusCode == http.StatusForbidden || resp.StatusCode == http.StatusTooManyRequests {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"github rate limit exceeded\")\n\t}\n\tif resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"GitHub API returned %d\", resp.StatusCode)\n\t}\n\n\tvar raw json.RawMessage\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&raw); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"decode GitHub response: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn raw, nil\n}\n\nfunc (h *SuggestionsHandler) Stars(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tuser := auth.UserFromContext(r.Context())\n\tif user == nil || user.GitHubLogin == \"\" || user.GitHubLogin == \"dev\" {\n\t\tRespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, []SuggestionItem{})\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tlogin := user.GitHubLogin\n\tcacheKey := \"stars:\" + login\n\n\t// Fetch from GitHub (or cache)\n\traw, ok := h.getCached(cacheKey)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\tvar err error\n\t\t// Fetch up to 4 pages (120 stars)\n\t\tvar allStars []json.RawMessage\n\t\tfor page := 1; page <= 4; page++ {\n\t\t\tpath := fmt.Sprintf(\"/users/%s/starred?per_page=30&page=%d\", login, page)\n\t\t\tpageRaw, fetchErr := h.githubGet(r.Context(), path)\n\t\t\tif fetchErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\tif page == 1 {\n\t\t\t\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadGateway, \"upstream_error\", fetchErr.Error())\n\t\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tbreak // partial results OK\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tvar pageItems []json.RawMessage\n\t\t\tif err = json.Unmarshal(pageRaw, &pageItems); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadGateway, \"upstream_error\", \"failed to parse GitHub response\")\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tallStars = append(allStars, pageItems...)\n\t\t\tif len(pageItems) < 30 {\n\t\t\t\tbreak // last page\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\traw, err = json.Marshal(allStars)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, \"internal_error\", \"failed to marshal stars\")\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\th.setCache(cacheKey, raw)\n\t}\n\n\t// Parse raw GitHub response\n\tvar ghStars []struct {\n\t\tName     string `json:\"name\"`\n\t\tFullName string `json:\"full_name\"`\n\t\tDesc     string `json:\"description\"`\n\t\tStars    int    `json:\"stargazers_count\"`\n\t\tLanguage string `json:\"language\"`\n\t\tURL      string `json:\"html_url\"`\n\t\tOwner    struct {\n\t\t\tAvatarURL string `json:\"avatar_url\"`\n\t\t} `json:\"owner\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &ghStars); err != nil {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, \"internal_error\", \"failed to parse cached stars\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Get tracked repos for filtering\n\ttracked := make(map[string]bool)\n\tif repos, err := h.store.ListAllSourceRepos(r.Context()); err == nil {\n\t\tfor _, repo := range repos {\n\t\t\ttracked[repo.Provider+\":\"+repo.Repository] = true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Map to response\n\titems := make([]SuggestionItem, 0, len(ghStars))\n\tfor _, s := range ghStars {\n\t\titems = append(items, SuggestionItem{\n\t\t\tName:      s.Name,\n\t\t\tFullName:  s.FullName,\n\t\t\tDescription: s.Desc,\n\t\t\tStars:     s.Stars,\n\t\t\tLanguage:  s.Language,\n\t\t\tURL:       s.URL,\n\t\t\tAvatarURL: s.Owner.AvatarURL,\n\t\t\tProvider:  \"github\",\n\t\t\tTracked:   tracked[\"github:\"+s.FullName],\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\tRespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, items)\n}\n\nfunc (h *SuggestionsHandler) Repos(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tuser := auth.UserFromContext(r.Context())\n\tif user == nil || user.GitHubLogin == \"\" || user.GitHubLogin == \"dev\" {\n\t\tRespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, []RepoItem{})\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tlogin := user.GitHubLogin\n\tcacheKey := \"repos:\" + login\n\n\traw, ok := h.getCached(cacheKey)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\tvar err error\n\t\traw, err = h.githubGet(r.Context(), fmt.Sprintf(\"/users/%s/repos?sort=pushed&per_page=100\", login))\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadGateway, \"upstream_error\", err.Error())\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\th.setCache(cacheKey, raw)\n\t}\n\n\tvar ghRepos []struct {\n\t\tName     string `json:\"name\"`\n\t\tFullName string `json:\"full_name\"`\n\t\tDesc     string `json:\"description\"`\n\t\tLanguage string `json:\"language\"`\n\t\tURL      string `json:\"html_url\"`\n\t\tPushedAt string `json:\"pushed_at\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &ghRepos); err != nil {\n\t\tRespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, \"internal_error\", \"failed to parse cached repos\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\titems := make([]RepoItem, 0, len(ghRepos))\n\tfor _, repo := range ghRepos {\n\t\titems = append(items, RepoItem{\n\t\t\tName:        repo.Name,\n\t\t\tFullName:    repo.FullName,\n\t\t\tDescription: repo.Desc,\n\t\t\tLanguage:    repo.Language,\n\t\t\tURL:         repo.URL,\n\t\t\tPushedAt:    repo.PushedAt,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\tRespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, items)\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 7: Run all tests**\n\nRun: `go test -v -run TestSuggestions `web/components/dashboard/stars-tab.tsx`:\n\n```tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState, useCallback } from \"react\";\nimport useSWR, { mutate } from \"swr\";\nimport { suggestions, projects, sources } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport type { SuggestionItem } from \"@/lib/api/types\";\n\nexport function StarsTab() {\n  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);\n  const [trackingIds, setTrackingIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());\n\n  const { data, isLoading, error } = useSWR(\n    loaded ? \"suggestions-stars\" : null,\n    () => suggestions.stars(),\n    { revalidateOnFocus: false }\n  );\n\n  const items = data?.data ?? [];\n\n  const handleTrack = useCallback(async (item: SuggestionItem) => {\n    const key = item.full_name;\n    setTrackingIds((prev) => new Set(prev).add(key));\n    try {\n      const projRes = await projects.create({\n        name: item.full_name,\n        description: item.description,\n      });\n      const project = projRes.data;\n      const sourceRes = await sources.create(project.id, {\n        provider: \"github\",\n        repository: item.full_name,\n        poll_interval_seconds: 86400,\n        enabled: true,\n      });\n      await sources.poll(sourceRes.data.id);\n      await mutate(\"suggestions-stars\");\n      await mutate(\"projects-list\");\n      await mutate(\"projects-for-dashboard\");\n    } catch (err: unknown) {\n      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : \"Failed to track\";\n      alert(msg);\n    } finally {\n      setTrackingIds((prev) => {\n        const next = new Set(prev);\n        next.delete(key);\n        return next;\n      });\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  if (!loaded) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-12\">\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setLoaded(true)}\n          className=\"rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-purple-700 transition-colors\"\n        >\n          Load your starred repos\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (isLoading) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-12\">\n        <div className=\"text-sm text-zinc-400\">Loading your starred repos...</div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (error) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-12\">\n        <div className=\"text-sm text-red-400\">Failed to load stars. Try again later.</div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (items.length === 0) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-12\">\n        <div className=\"text-sm text-zinc-400\">\n          You haven&apos;t starred any public repos on GitHub yet.\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3\">\n      {items.map((item) => (\n        <div\n          key={item.full_name}\n          className={`rounded-lg border p-3 ${\n            item.tracked\n              ? \"border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800/50 opacity-60\"\n              : \"border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800\"\n          }`}\n        >\n          <div className=\"flex items-start justify-between gap-2 mb-2\">\n            <span className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-200 truncate\">\n              {item.full_name}\n            </span>\n            {item.tracked ? (\n              <span className=\"shrink-0 text-xs text-green-400\">✓ Tracked</span>\n            ) : (\n              <button\n                onClick={() => handleTrack(item)}\n                disabled={trackingIds.has(item.full_name)}\n                className=\"shrink-0 rounded bg-purple-600 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-white hover:bg-purple-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors\"\n              >\n                {trackingIds.has(item.full_name) ? \"Tracking...\" : \"Track\"}\n              </button>\n            )}\n          </div>\n          {item.description && (\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-zinc-400 mb-2 line-clamp-2\">\n              {item.description}\n            </p>\n          )}\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-zinc-500\">\n            <span>⭐ {item.stars.toLocaleString()}</span>\n            {item.language && <span>● {item.language}</span>}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      ))}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Verify no TypeScript errors**\n\nRun: `cd web && npx tsc --noEmit`\nExpected: No errors (or only pre-existing errors)\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add web/components/dashboard/stars-tab.tsx\ngit commit -m \"feat(web): add StarsTab component for GitHub starred repos\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Task 6: Dependencies tab component\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `web/components/dashboard/deps-tab.tsx`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Create DepsTab component**\n\nCreate `web/components/dashboard/deps-tab.tsx`:\n\n```tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState, useCallback } from \"react\";\nimport useSWR from \"swr\";\nimport { suggestions, onboard } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport type { RepoItem, OnboardScan, ScannedDependency, OnboardSelection } from \"@/lib/api/types\";\n\ntype Step = \"pick\" | \"scanning\" | \"results\" | \"applied\";\n\ninterface ScanState {\n  repoName: string;\n  scanId: string;\n  status: string;\n  results?: ScannedDependency[];\n  error?: string;\n}\n\nexport function DepsTab() {\n  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);\n  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>(\"pick\");\n  const [selectedRepos, setSelectedRepos] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());\n  const [scans, setScans] = useState<ScanState[]>([]);\n  const [appliedCount, setAppliedCount] = useState(0);\n\n  const { data, isLoading, error } = useSWR(\n    loaded ? \"suggestions-repos\" : null,\n    () => suggestions.repos(),\n    { revalidateOnFocus: false }\n  );\n\n  const repos = data?.data ?? [];\n\n  const toggleRepo = useCallback((fullName: string) => {\n    setSelectedRepos((prev) => {\n      const next = new Set(prev);\n      if (next.has(fullName)) next.delete(fullName);\n      else next.add(fullName);\n      return next;\n    });\n  }, []);\n\n  const handleScan = useCallback(async () => {\n    const repoNames = Array.from(selectedRepos);\n    setStep(\"scanning\");\n\n    const scanStates: ScanState[] = repoNames.map((name) => ({\n      repoName: name,\n      scanId: \"\",\n      status: \"queued\",\n    }));\n    setScans([...scanStates]);\n\n    // Start scans (max 5 concurrent)\n    const batchSize = 5;\n    for (let i = 0; i < repoNames.length; i += batchSize) {\n      const batch = repoNames.slice(i, i + batchSize);\n      await Promise.all(\n        batch.map(async (repoName, batchIdx) => {\n          const idx = i + batchIdx;\n          try {\n            const repoUrl = `https://github.com/${repoName}`;\n            const scanRes = await onboard.scan(repoUrl);\n            const scanId = scanRes.data.id;\n            scanStates[idx] = { ...scanStates[idx], scanId, status: \"processing\" };\n            setScans([...scanStates]);\n\n            // Poll until complete\n            let scan: OnboardScan;\n            do {\n              await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2000));\n              const pollRes = await onboard.getScan(scanId);\n              scan = pollRes.data;\n            } while (scan.status === \"pending\" || scan.status === \"processing\");\n\n            if (scan.status === \"completed\" && scan.results) {\n              scanStates[idx] = { ...scanStates[idx], status: \"completed\", results: scan.results };\n            } else {\n              scanStates[idx] = { ...scanStates[idx], status: \"failed\", error: scan.error || \"Unknown error\" };\n            }\n          } catch (err: unknown) {\n            const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : \"Scan failed\";\n            scanStates[idx] = { ...scanStates[idx], status: \"failed\", error: msg };\n          }\n          setScans([...scanStates]);\n        })\n      );\n    }\n\n    setStep(\"results\");\n  }, [selectedRepos]);\n\n  const handleApply = useCallback(\n    async (selections: { scanId: string; items: OnboardSelection[] }[]) => {\n      let total = 0;\n      for (const { scanId, items } of selections) {\n        if (items.length === 0) continue;\n        try {\n          const res = await onboard.apply(scanId, items);\n          total += res.data.sources_created;\n        } catch {\n          // continue with other scans\n        }\n      }\n      setAppliedCount(total);\n      setStep(\"applied\");\n    },\n    []\n  );\n\n  if (!loaded) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-12\">\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setLoaded(true)}\n          className=\"rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-purple-700 transition-colors\"\n        >\n          Load your repos\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (isLoading) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-12\">\n        <div className=\"text-sm text-zinc-400\">Loading your repos...</div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (error) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-12\">\n        <div className=\"text-sm text-red-400\">Failed to load repos. Try again later.</div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (step === \"pick\") {\n    return <RepoPicker repos={repos} selected={selectedRepos} onToggle={toggleRepo} onScan={handleScan} />;\n  }\n\n  if (step === \"scanning\") {\n    return <ScanProgress scans={scans} />;\n  }\n\n  if (step === \"results\") {\n    return <ScanResults scans={scans.filter((s) => s.status === \"completed\")} onApply={handleApply} />;\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-12 gap-3\">\n      <div className=\"text-sm text-green-400\">\n        Successfully started tracking {appliedCount} dependencies.\n      </div>\n      <button\n        onClick={() => {\n          setStep(\"pick\");\n          setScans([]);\n          setSelectedRepos(new Set());\n        }}\n        className=\"text-xs text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200 underline\"\n      >\n        Scan more repos\n      </button>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction RepoPicker({\n  repos,\n  selected,\n  onToggle,\n  onScan,\n}: {\n  repos: RepoItem[];\n  selected: Set<string>;\n  onToggle: (name: string) => void;\n  onScan: () => void;\n}) {\n  if (repos.length === 0) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-12\">\n        <div className=\"text-sm text-zinc-400\">No public repos found.</div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div>\n      <p className=\"text-sm text-zinc-400 mb-3\">Select repos to scan for dependencies:</p>\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto\">\n        {repos.map((repo) => (\n          <label\n            key={repo.full_name}\n            className=\"flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800 p-3 cursor-pointer hover:border-zinc-600 transition-colors\"\n          >\n            <input\n              type=\"checkbox\"\n              checked={selected.has(repo.full_name)}\n              onChange={() => onToggle(repo.full_name)}\n              className=\"accent-purple-600\"\n            />\n            <div className=\"flex-1 min-w-0\">\n              <div className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-200 truncate\">{repo.full_name}</div>\n              <div className=\"text-xs text-zinc-500\">\n                {repo.pushed_at && `Pushed ${new Date(repo.pushed_at).toLocaleDateString()}`}\n                {repo.language && ` · ${repo.language}`}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </label>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"flex justify-end mt-3\">\n        <button\n          onClick={onScan}\n          disabled={selected.size === 0}\n          className=\"rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-purple-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors\"\n        >\n          Scan Selected ({selected.size})\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction ScanProgress({ scans }: { scans: ScanState[] }) {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-2 py-4\">\n      <p className=\"text-sm text-zinc-400 mb-3\">Scanning repositories for dependencies...</p>\n      {scans.map((scan) => (\n        <div key={scan.repoName} className=\"flex items-center gap-3 text-sm\">\n          <span className=\"w-5 text-center\">\n            {scan.status === \"completed\" && \"✓\"}\n            {scan.status === \"failed\" && \"✗\"}\n            {(scan.status === \"queued\" || scan.status === \"processing\") && \"⏳\"}\n          </span>\n          <span className={scan.status === \"failed\" ? \"text-red-400\" : \"text-zinc-300\"}>\n            {scan.repoName}\n          </span>\n          {scan.status === \"failed\" && scan.error && (\n            <span className=\"text-xs text-red-400/70\">({scan.error})</span>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      ))}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction ScanResults({\n  scans,\n  onApply,\n}: {\n  scans: ScanState[];\n  onApply: (selections: { scanId: string; items: OnboardSelection[] }[]) => void;\n}) {\n  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<string>>(() => {\n    const all = new Set<string>();\n    scans.forEach((scan) =>\n      scan.results?.forEach((dep) => all.add(`${scan.scanId}:${dep.name}`))\n    );\n    return all;\n  });\n\n  const toggle = (key: string) => {\n    setSelected((prev) => {\n      const next = new Set(prev);\n      if (next.has(key)) next.delete(key);\n      else next.add(key);\n      return next;\n    });\n  };\n\n  const handleApply = () => {\n    const selections = scans.map((scan) => ({\n      scanId: scan.scanId,\n      items: (scan.results ?? [])\n        .filter((dep) => selected.has(`${scan.scanId}:${dep.name}`))\n        .map((dep) => ({\n          dep_name: dep.name,\n          upstream_repo: dep.upstream_repo,\n          provider: dep.provider,\n          new_project_name: dep.upstream_repo || dep.name,\n        })),\n    }));\n    onApply(selections);\n  };\n\n  const totalSelected = selected.size;\n\n  if (scans.length === 0 || scans.every((s) => !s.results?.length)) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-12\">\n        <div className=\"text-sm text-zinc-400\">No dependencies found in the selected repos.</div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div>\n      {scans.map((scan) => {\n        if (!scan.results?.length) return null;\n        return (\n          <div key={scan.scanId} className=\"mb-4\">\n            <h4 className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-300 mb-2\">\n              From {scan.repoName}\n            </h4>\n            <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1\">\n              {scan.results.map((dep) => {\n                const key = `${scan.scanId}:${dep.name}`;\n                return (\n                  <label\n                    key={key}\n                    className=\"flex items-center gap-3 rounded border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800/50 px-3 py-2 cursor-pointer text-sm\"\n                  >\n                    <input\n                      type=\"checkbox\"\n                      checked={selected.has(key)}\n                      onChange={() => toggle(key)}\n                      className=\"accent-purple-600\"\n                    />\n                    <span className=\"text-zinc-200\">{dep.name}</span>\n                    {dep.version && (\n                      <span className=\"text-xs text-zinc-500\">{dep.version}</span>\n                    )}\n                    <span className=\"ml-auto text-xs text-zinc-500\">{dep.ecosystem}</span>\n                  </label>\n                );\n              })}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        );\n      })}\n      <div className=\"flex justify-end mt-3\">\n        <button\n          onClick={handleApply}\n          disabled={totalSelected === 0}\n          className=\"rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-purple-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors\"\n        >\n          Track Selected ({totalSelected})\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Verify no TypeScript errors**\n\nRun: `cd web && npx tsc --noEmit`\nExpected: No from \"./stars-tab\";\nimport { DepsTab } from \"./deps-tab\";\n\ntype Tab = \"stars\" | \"deps\";\n\nexport function SuggestionsSection() {\n  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>(\"stars\");\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"rounded-xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900 p-4\">\n      <div className=\"flex gap-0 mb-4 border-b border-zinc-700\">\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setTab(\"stars\")}\n          className={`px-5 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${\n            tab === \"stars\"\n              ? \"border-b-2 border-purple-500 text-purple-400\"\n              : \"text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200\"\n          }`}\n        >\n          ⭐ Your Stars\n        </button>\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setTab(\"deps\")}\n          className={`px-5 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${\n            tab === \"deps\"\n              ? \"border-b-2 border-purple-500 text-purple-400\"\n              : \"text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200\"\n          }`}\n        >\n          📦 Your Dependencies\n        </button>\n      </div>\n      {tab === \"stars\" ? <StarsTab /> : <DepsTab />}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Update dashboard page to conditionally render**\n\nIn `web/app/page.tsx`, replace the `<DiscoverySection />` usage with conditional rendering:\n\n```tsx\nimport { SuggestionsSection } from I now have a complete picture. Here is my review.

---

## Issues Found

### 1. PgStore field name mismatch: `s.db` should be `s.pool`

In Task 1, Step 2, the `ListAllSourceRepos` implementation uses `s.db.Query(...)`:

```go
rows, err := s.db.Query(ctx, `SELECT DISTINCT provider, repository FROM sources WHERE enabled = true`)
```

The actual `PgStore` struct (in `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/pgstore.go`, line 22) uses `pool`, not `db`:

```go
type PgStore struct {
    pool  *pgxpool.Pool
    river *river.Client[pgx.Tx]
}
```

All existing methods use `s.pool.Query(...)`. This must be changed to `s.pool.Query(...)`.

---

### 2. `mockSourcesStore` in `sources_test.go` will fail compilation

Task 1, Step 3 adds `ListAllSourceRepos` to the mock in `sources_test.go`. However, the existing `mockSourcesStore` in `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/sources_test.go` implements the `SourcesStore` interface. Adding `ListAllSourceRepos` to `SourcesStore` will break this mock at compile time **unless** the method is added to that mock too. The plan does say "Add to mock in sources_test.go", which is correct, but it should be verified that this is added to the **existing** `mockSourcesStore` struct (which the plan correctly targets).

This is fine as described. No issue here -- disregard.

---

### 3. `SuggestionsHandler` constructor signature does not match spec

The spec says:

> `NewSuggestionsHandler(httpClient *http.Client, sourcesStore db.SourcesStore, githubToken string)`

The plan's implementation has 4 parameters:

```go
func NewSuggestionsHandler(client *http.Client, store SuggestionsSourceStore, token, githubURL string) *SuggestionsHandler
```

This is actually **better** than the spec because it allows URL injection for testing (matching `DiscoverHandler` pattern). This is an improvement, not a bug. **No action needed.**

---

### 4. `RespondJSON` wraps data in `{ data: ... }` envelope but test decodes raw struct

The test in Task 2 (TestSuggestionsStars) decodes the response as:

```go
var got struct {
    Data []SuggestionItem `json:"data"`
}
```

And `RespondJSON` wraps with an `envelope{Data: data, Meta: ...}`. This is consistent. **No issue.**

---

### 5. API key auth returns 403 per spec, but plan returns empty array instead

The spec states:

> API key auth (Bearer token): returns 403 -- these endpoints require a session with a GitHub identity

The plan's handler implementation does **not** distinguish between API key auth and no-user context. Both return an empty array with 200 OK. If the spec requirement for 403 on API key auth is important, the handler needs to detect API-key-only callers and return 403. However, the plan's approach (return empty gracefully) is arguably simpler and still functional. This is a **spec deviation** that should be called out.

---

### 6. `OnboardApplyResult` does not have `sources_created` field

In Task 6 (DepsTab), the `handleApply` function references:

```typescript
total += res.data.sources_created;
```

But looking at `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/types.ts` (lines 305-309), `OnboardApplyResult` is:

```typescript
export interface OnboardApplyResult {
  created_projects: Project[];
  created_sources: Source[];
  skipped: string[];
}
```

There is **no** `sources_created` numeric field. The correct reference would be `res.data.created_sources.length`. This is a **runtime bug** that will cause NaN or undefined behavior.

---

### 7. Plan modifies `web/lib/api/types.ts` but does not modify `internal/api/pgstore.go` file listing

The plan's "Modified files" table at the top lists:

> `internal/api/pgstore.go` | Add `ListAllSourceRepos()` method

But does **not** list `internal/models/source.go` in the "Modified files" table, even though Task 1 Step 1 adds `SourceRepo` to that file. The file structure table should list `internal/models/source.go` under "Modified files".

---

### 8. `page.tsx` integration needs `useAuth` import but the page is a `"use client"` component that does not currently import it

Task 7 Step 2 instructs:

```tsx
import { useAuth } from "@/lib/auth/context";
const { user } = useAuth();
```

Looking at `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/page.tsx`, the page currently does not use `useAuth`. The plan correctly identifies this import needs to be added. However, it is vague: "replace the `<DiscoverySection />` usage with conditional rendering" without specifying the exact edit. The current code on line 71 is:

```tsx
<DiscoverySection />
```

The plan must also add the `SuggestionsSection` import. This is mentioned but the instructions should be more precise about the exact line edits. This is a minor clarity issue, not a blocker.

---

### 9. Spec mentions "Show more" pagination for StarsTab -- not implemented in plan

The spec says:

> "Show more" pagination (client-side, data already fetched)

The plan's `StarsTab` renders all items in a grid without any pagination or "Show more" button. If the user has 120 starred repos (4 pages), they all render at once. This is a minor spec gap -- not a blocker, but worth noting.

---

### 10. Spec mentions dropdown for "assign to existing project or create new one" in DepsTab results -- not implemented

The spec says:

> Dropdown to assign to existing project or create new one.

The plan's `ScanResults` component only has checkboxes and a "Track Selected" button, with no project assignment dropdown. The `handleApply` creates `new_project_name` automatically from `dep.upstream_repo || dep.name`. This is a simplification from the spec.

---

### 11. Spec mentions "already-tracked dependencies" indication in DepsTab results -- not implemented

The spec says:

> Deps tab results similarly indicate already-tracked dependencies.

The plan's `ScanResults` component does not check or display whether scanned dependencies are already tracked. The `Stars` endpoint does this via the `tracked` flag, but the deps flow does not.

---

### 12. Missing `"Show more" pagination` edge cases in Stars tab and no empty-state fallback suggestion

The spec says for empty states:

> Empty state messages with suggestion to try trending discovery or quick onboard instead.

The plan's empty state just says "You haven't starred any public repos on GitHub yet." without suggesting alternatives.

---

### 13. Stars pagination: plan fetches up to 4 pages (120 items) but spec says "~100 results"

The plan says "Fetch up to 4 pages (120 stars)" in the handler code, while the spec says "Paginates up to ~100 results (GitHub returns 30/page, fetch up to 4 pages)". This is internally consistent within the spec ("fetch up to 4 pages" = 120), so the plan is actually following the spec correctly. **No issue.**

---

### Summary

**Critical issues (will cause compilation errors or runtime bugs):**

1. **Issue #1**: `s.db.Query` must be `s.pool.Query` in `ListAllSourceRepos` -- compilation error.
2. **Issue #6**: `res.data.sources_created` does not exist on `OnboardApplyResult` -- should be `res.data.created_sources.length` -- runtime bug.

**Spec deviations (functional but not matching spec):**

3. **Issue #5**: API key auth should return 403 per spec, but plan returns empty array.
4. **Issue #9**: Missing "Show more" pagination on StarsTab.
5. **Issue #10**: Missing project assignment dropdown in DepsTab results.
6. **Issue #11**: DepsTab does not indicate already-tracked dependencies.
7. **Issue #12**: Empty states missing fallback suggestions.

**Documentation/clarity:**

8. **Issue #7**: `internal/models/source.go` not listed in the "Modified files" table.
9. **Issue #8**: `page.tsx` integration instructions could be more precise with exact line edits.

**Verdict: Issues Found.** The two critical issues (#1 and #6) must be fixed before execution. The spec deviations (#5, #9, #10, #11, #12) should be explicitly acknowledged as "deferred" or fixed in the plan.

    70→- [ ] **Step 2: Implement in PgStore**

    72→In `internal/api/pgstore.go`, add after the `UpdateSourcePollStatus` method:
    73→
    74→```go
    75→func (s *PgStore) ListAllSourceRepos(ctx context.Context) ([]models.SourceRepo, error) {
    76→	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx, `SELECT DISTINCT provider, repository FROM sources WHERE enabled = true`)
    77→	if err != nil {
    78→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("list all source repos: %w", err)
    79→	}
    80→	defer rows.Close()
    81→	var repos []models.SourceRepo
    82→	for rows.Next() {
    83→		var r models.SourceRepo
    84→		if err := rows.Scan(&r.Provider, &r.Repository); err != nil {
    85→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("scan source repo: %w", err)
    86→		}
    87→		repos = append(repos, r)
    88→	}
    89→	return repos, rows.Err()
    90→}
    91→```

   346→- [ ] **Step 6: Implement SuggestionsHandler**
   347→
   348→Create `internal/api/suggestions.go`:
   349→
   350→```go
   351→package api
   352→
   353→import (
   354→	"context"
   355→	"encoding/json"
   356→	"fmt"
   357→	"net/http"
   358→	"os"
   359→	"sync"
   360→	"time"
   361→
   362→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/auth"
   363→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
   364→)
   365→
   366→const suggestionsCacheTTL = 1 * time.Hour
   367→
   368→type SuggestionItem struct {
   369→	Name        string `json:"name"`
   370→	FullName    string `json:"full_name"`
   371→	Description string `json:"description"`
   372→	Stars       int    `json:"stars"`
   373→	Language    string `json:"language,omitempty"`
   374→	URL         string `json:"url"`
   375→	AvatarURL   string `json:"avatar_url,omitempty"`
   376→	Provider    string `json:"provider"`
   377→	Tracked     bool   `json:"tracked"`
   378→}
   379→
   380→type RepoItem struct {
   381→	Name        string `json:"name"`
   382→	FullName    string `json:"full_name"`
   383→	Description string `json:"description"`
   384→	Language    string `json:"language,omitempty"`
   385→	URL         string `json:"url"`
   386→	PushedAt    string `json:"pushed_at"`
   387→}
   388→
   389→type SuggestionsSourceStore interface {
   390→	ListAllSourceRepos(ctx context.Context) ([]models.SourceRepo, error)
   391→}
   392→
   393→type suggestionsCache struct {
   394→	data      json.RawMessage
   395→	fetchedAt time.Time
   396→}
   397→
   398→type SuggestionsHandler struct {
   399→	client    *http.Client
   400→	store     SuggestionsSourceStore
   401→	token     string
   402→	githubURL string
   403→	mu        sync.Mutex
   404→	cache     map[string]suggestionsCache
   405→}
   406→
   407→func NewSuggestionsHandler(client *http.Client, store SuggestionsSourceStore, token, githubURL string) *SuggestionsHandler {
   408→	if token == "" {
   409→		token = os.Getenv("GITHUB_TOKEN")
   410→	}
   411→	if githubURL == "" {
   412→		githubURL = "https://api.github.com"
   413→	}
   414→	return &SuggestionsHandler{
   415→		client:    client,
   416→		store:     store,
   417→		token:     token,
   418→		githubURL: githubURL,
   419→		cache:     make(map[string]suggestionsCache),
   420→	}
   421→}
   422→
   423→func (h *SuggestionsHandler) getCached(key string) (json.RawMessage, bool) {
   424→	h.mu.Lock()
   425→	defer h.mu.Unlock()
   426→	c, ok := h.cache[key]
   427→	if !ok {
   428→		return nil, false
   429→	}
   430→	if time.Since(c.fetchedAt) > suggestionsCacheTTL {
   431→		delete(h.cache, key)
   432→		return nil, false
   433→	}
   434→	return c.data, true
   435→}
   436→
   437→func (h *SuggestionsHandler) setCache(key string, data json.RawMessage) {
   438→	h.mu.Lock()
   439→	defer h.mu.Unlock()
   440→	h.cache[key] = suggestionsCache{data: data, fetchedAt: time.Now()}
   441→}
   442→
   443→func (h *SuggestionsHandler) githubGet(ctx context.Context, path string) (json.RawMessage, error) {
   444→	url := h.githubURL + path
   445→	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, "GET", url, nil)
   446→	if err != nil {
   447→		return nil, err
   448→	}
   449→	if h.token != "" {
   450→		req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+h.token)
   451→	}
   452→	req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/vnd.github+json")
   453→
   454→	resp, err := h.client.Do(req)
   455→	if err != nil {
   456→		return nil, err
   457→	}
   458→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   459→
   460→	if resp.StatusCode == http.StatusForbidden || resp.StatusCode == http.StatusTooManyRequests {
   461→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("github rate limit exceeded")
   462→	}
   463→	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
   464→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("GitHub API returned %d", resp.StatusCode)
   465→	}
   466→
   467→	var raw json.RawMessage
   468→	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&raw); err != nil {
   469→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("decode GitHub response: %w", err)
   470→	}
   471→	return raw, nil
   472→}
   473→
   474→func (h *SuggestionsHandler) Stars(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   475→	user := auth.UserFromContext(r.Context())
   476→	if user == nil || user.GitHubLogin == "" || user.GitHubLogin == "dev" {
   477→		RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, []SuggestionItem{})
   478→		return
   479→	}
   480→
   481→	login := user.GitHubLogin
   482→	cacheKey := "stars:" + login
   483→
   484→	// Fetch from GitHub (or cache)
   485→	raw, ok := h.getCached(cacheKey)
   486→	if !ok {
   487→		var err error
   488→		// Fetch up to 4 pages (120 stars)
   489→		var allStars []json.RawMessage
   490→		for page := 1; page <= 4; page++ {
   491→			path := fmt.Sprintf("/users/%s/starred?per_page=30&page=%d", login, page)
   492→			pageRaw, fetchErr := h.githubGet(r.Context(), path)
   493→			if fetchErr != nil {
   494→				if page == 1 {
   495→					RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadGateway, "upstream_error", fetchErr.Error())
   496→					return
   497→				}
   498→				break // partial results OK
   499→			}
   500→			var pageItems []json.RawMessage
   501→			if err = json.Unmarshal(pageRaw, &pageItems); err != nil {
   502→				RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadGateway, "upstream_error", "failed to parse GitHub response")
   503→				return
   504→			}
   505→			allStars = append(allStars, pageItems...)
   506→			if len(pageItems) < 30 {
   507→				break // last page
   508→			}
   509→		}
   510→		raw, err = json.Marshal(allStars)
   511→		if err != nil {
   512→			RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "failed to marshal stars")
   513→			return
   514→		}
   515→		h.setCache(cacheKey, raw)
   516→	}
   517→
   518→	// Parse raw GitHub response
   519→	var ghStars []struct {
   520→		Name     string `json:"name"`
   521→		FullName string `json:"full_name"`
   522→		Desc     string `json:"description"`
   523→		Stars    int    `json:"stargazers_count"`
   524→		Language string `json:"language"`
   525→		URL      string `json:"html_url"`
   526→		Owner    struct {
   527→			AvatarURL string `json:"avatar_url"`
   528→		} `json:"owner"`
   529→	}
   530→	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &ghStars); err != nil {
   531→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "failed to parse cached stars")
   532→		return
   533→	}
   534→
   535→	// Get tracked repos for filtering
   536→	tracked := make(map[string]bool)
   537→	if repos, err := h.store.ListAllSourceRepos(r.Context()); err == nil {
   538→		for _, repo := range repos {
   539→			tracked[repo.Provider+":"+repo.Repository] = true
   540→		}
   541→	}
   542→
   543→	// Map to response
   544→	items := make([]SuggestionItem, 0, len(ghStars))
   545→	for _, s := range ghStars {
   546→		items = append(items, SuggestionItem{
   547→			Name:      s.Name,
   548→			FullName:  s.FullName,
   549→			Description: s.Desc,
   550→			Stars:     s.Stars,
   551→			Language:  s.Language,
   552→			URL:       s.URL,
   553→			AvatarURL: s.Owner.AvatarURL,
   554→			Provider:  "github",
   555→			Tracked:   tracked["github:"+s.FullName],
   556→		})
   557→	}
   558→
   559→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, items)
   560→}
   561→
   562→func (h *SuggestionsHandler) Repos(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   563→	user := auth.UserFromContext(r.Context())
   564→	if user == nil || user.GitHubLogin == "" || user.GitHubLogin == "dev" {
   565→		RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, []RepoItem{})
   566→		return
   567→	}
   568→
   569→	login := user.GitHubLogin
   570→	cacheKey := "repos:" + login
   571→
   572→	raw, ok := h.getCached(cacheKey)
   573→	if !ok {
   574→		var err error
   575→		raw, err = h.githubGet(r.Context(), fmt.Sprintf("/users/%s/repos?sort=pushed&per_page=100", login))
   576→		if err != nil {
   577→			RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadGateway, "upstream_error", err.Error())
   578→			return
   579→		}
   580→		h.setCache(cacheKey, raw)
   581→	}
   582→
   583→	var ghRepos []struct {
   584→		Name     string `json:"name"`
   585→		FullName string `json:"full_name"`
   586→		Desc     string `json:"description"`
   587→		Language string `json:"language"`
   588→		URL      string `json:"html_url"`
   589→		PushedAt string `json:"pushed_at"`
   590→	}
   591→	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &ghRepos); err != nil {
   592→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "failed to parse cached repos")
   593→		return
   594→	}
   595→
   596→	items := make([]RepoItem, 0, len(ghRepos))
   597→	for _, repo := range ghRepos {
   598→		items = append(items, RepoItem{
   599→			Name:        repo.Name,
   600→			FullName:    repo.FullName,
   601→			Description: repo.Desc,
   602→			Language:    repo.Language,
   603→			URL:         repo.URL,
   604→			PushedAt:    repo.PushedAt,
   605→		})
   606→	}
   607→
   608→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, items)
   609→}
   610→```

   715→## Task 5: Stars tab component
   716→
   717→**Files:**
   718→- Create: `web/components/dashboard/stars-tab.tsx`
   719→
   720→- [ ] **Step 1: Create StarsTab component**
   721→
   722→Create `web/components/dashboard/stars-tab.tsx`:
   723→
   724→```tsx
   725→"use client";
   726→
   727→import { useState, useCallback } from "react";
   728→import useSWR, { mutate } from "swr";
   729→import { suggestions, projects, sources } from "@/lib/api/client";
   730→import type { SuggestionItem } from "@/lib/api/types";
   731→
   732→export function StarsTab() {
   733→  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
   734→  const [trackingIds, setTrackingIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
   735→
   736→  const { data, isLoading, error } = useSWR(
   737→    loaded ? "suggestions-stars" : null,
   738→    () => suggestions.stars(),
   739→    { revalidateOnFocus: false }
   740→  );
   741→
   742→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
   743→
   744→  const handleTrack = useCallback(async (item: SuggestionItem) => {
   745→    const key = item.full_name;
   746→    setTrackingIds((prev) => new Set(prev).add(key));
   747→    try {
   748→      const projRes = await projects.create({
   749→        name: item.full_name,
   750→        description: item.description,
   751→      });
   752→      const project = projRes.data;
   753→      const sourceRes = await sources.create(project.id, {
   754→        provider: "github",
   755→        repository: item.full_name,
   756→        poll_interval_seconds: 86400,
   757→        enabled: true,
   758→      });
   759→      await sources.poll(sourceRes.data.id);
   760→      await mutate("suggestions-stars");
   761→      await mutate("projects-list");
   762→      await mutate("projects-for-dashboard");
   763→    } catch (err: unknown) {
   764→      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to track";
   765→      alert(msg);
   766→    } finally {
   767→      setTrackingIds((prev) => {
   768→        const next = new Set(prev);
   769→        next.delete(key);
   770→        return next;
   771→      });
   772→    }
   773→  }, []);
   774→
   775→  if (!loaded) {
   776→    return (
   777→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
   778→        <button
   779→          onClick={() => setLoaded(true)}
   780→          className="rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-purple-700 transition-colors"
   781→        >
   782→          Load your starred repos
   783→        </button>
   784→      </div>
   785→    );
   786→  }
   787→
   788→  if (isLoading) {
   789→    return (
   790→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
   791→        <div className="text-sm text-zinc-400">Loading your starred repos...</div>
   792→      </div>
   793→    );
   794→  }
   795→
   796→  if (error) {
   797→    return (
   798→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
   799→        <div className="text-sm text-red-400">Failed to load stars. Try again later.</div>
   800→      </div>
   801→    );
   802→  }
   803→
   804→  if (items.length === 0) {
   805→    return (
   806→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
   807→        <div className="text-sm text-zinc-400">
   808→          You haven&apos;t starred any public repos on GitHub yet.
   809→        </div>
   810→      </div>
   811→    );
   812→  }
   813→
   814→  return (
   815→    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
   816→      {items.map((item) => (
   817→        <div
   818→          key={item.full_name}
   819→          className={`rounded-lg border p-3 ${
   820→            item.tracked
   821→              ? "border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800/50 opacity-60"
   822→              : "border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800"
   823→          }`}
   824→        >
   825→          <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 mb-2">
   826→            <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-200 truncate">
   827→              {item.full_name}
   828→            </span>
   829→            {item.tracked ? (
   830→              <span className="shrink-0 text-xs text-green-400">✓ Tracked</span>
   831→            ) : (
   832→              <button
   833→                onClick={() => handleTrack(item)}
   834→                disabled={trackingIds.has(item.full_name)}
   835→                className="shrink-0 rounded bg-purple-600 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-white hover:bg-purple-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
   836→              >
   837→                {trackingIds.has(item.full_name) ? "Tracking..." : "Track"}
   838→              </button>
   839→            )}
   840→          </div>
   841→          {item.description && (
   842→            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mb-2 line-clamp-2">
   843→              {item.description}
   844→            </p>
   845→          )}
   846→          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-zinc-500">
   847→            <span>⭐ {item.stars.toLocaleString()}</span>
   848→            {item.language && <span>● {item.language}</span>}
   849→          </div>
   850→        </div>
   851→      ))}
   852→    </div>
   853→  );
   854→}
   855→```
   856→

   860→Expected: No errors (or only pre-existing errors)

   865→git add web/components/dashboard/stars-tab.tsx
   866→git commit -m "feat(web): add StarsTab component for GitHub starred repos"
   867→```

   871→## Task 6: Dependencies tab component
   872→
   873→**Files:**
   874→- Create: `web/components/dashboard/deps-tab.tsx`
   875→
   876→- [ ] **Step 1: Create DepsTab component**
   877→
   878→Create `web/components/dashboard/deps-tab.tsx`:
   879→
   880→```tsx
   881→"use client";
   882→
   883→import { useState, useCallback } from "react";
   884→import useSWR from "swr";
   885→import { suggestions, onboard } from "@/lib/api/client";
   886→import type { RepoItem, OnboardScan, ScannedDependency, OnboardSelection } from "@/lib/api/types";
   887→
   888→type Step = "pick" | "scanning" | "results" | "applied";
   889→
   890→interface ScanState {
   891→  repoName: string;
   892→  scanId: string;
   893→  status: string;
   894→  results?: ScannedDependency[];
   895→  error?: string;
   896→}
   897→
   898→export function DepsTab() {
   899→  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
   900→  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>("pick");
   901→  const [selectedRepos, setSelectedRepos] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
   902→  const [scans, setScans] = useState<ScanState[]>([]);
   903→  const [appliedCount, setAppliedCount] = useState(0);
   904→
   905→  const { data, isLoading, error } = useSWR(
   906→    loaded ? "suggestions-repos" : null,
   907→    () => suggestions.repos(),
   908→    { revalidateOnFocus: false }
   909→  );
   910→
   911→  const repos = data?.data ?? [];
   912→
   913→  const toggleRepo = useCallback((fullName: string) => {
   914→    setSelectedRepos((prev) => {
   915→      const next = new Set(prev);
   916→      if (next.has(fullName)) next.delete(fullName);
   917→      else next.add(fullName);
   918→      return next;
   919→    });
   920→  }, []);
   921→
   922→  const handleScan = useCallback(async () => {
   923→    const repoNames = Array.from(selectedRepos);
   924→    setStep("scanning");
   925→
   926→    const scanStates: ScanState[] = repoNames.map((name) => ({
   927→      repoName: name,
   928→      scanId: "",
   929→      status: "queued",
   930→    }));
   931→    setScans([...scanStates]);
   932→
   933→    // Start scans (max 5 concurrent)
   934→    const batchSize = 5;
   935→    for (let i = 0; i < repoNames.length; i += batchSize) {
   936→      const batch = repoNames.slice(i, i + batchSize);
   937→      await Promise.all(
   938→        batch.map(async (repoName, batchIdx) => {
   939→          const idx = i + batchIdx;
   940→          try {
   941→            const repoUrl = `https://github.com/${repoName}`;
   942→            const scanRes = await onboard.scan(repoUrl);
   943→            const scanId = scanRes.data.id;
   944→            scanStates[idx] = { ...scanStates[idx], scanId, status: "processing" };
   945→            setScans([...scanStates]);
   946→
   947→            // Poll until complete
   948→            let scan: OnboardScan;
   949→            do {
   950→              await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2000));
   951→              const pollRes = await onboard.getScan(scanId);
   952→              scan = pollRes.data;
   953→            } while (scan.status === "pending" || scan.status === "processing");
   954→
   955→            if (scan.status === "completed" && scan.results) {
   956→              scanStates[idx] = { ...scanStates[idx], status: "completed", results: scan.results };
   957→            } else {
   958→              scanStates[idx] = { ...scanStates[idx], status: "failed", error: scan.error || "Unknown error" };
   959→            }
   960→          } catch (err: unknown) {
   961→            const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Scan failed";
   962→            scanStates[idx] = { ...scanStates[idx], status: "failed", error: msg };
   963→          }
   964→          setScans([...scanStates]);
   965→        })
   966→      );
   967→    }
   968→
   969→    setStep("results");
   970→  }, [selectedRepos]);
   971→
   972→  const handleApply = useCallback(
   973→    async (selections: { scanId: string; items: OnboardSelection[] }[]) => {
   974→      let total = 0;
   975→      for (const { scanId, items } of selections) {
   976→        if (items.length === 0) continue;
   977→        try {
   978→          const res = await onboard.apply(scanId, items);
   979→          total += res.data.created_sources.length;
   980→        } catch {
   981→          // continue with other scans
   982→        }
   983→      }
   984→      setAppliedCount(total);
   985→      setStep("applied");
   986→    },
   987→    []
   988→  );
   989→
   990→  if (!loaded) {
   991→    return (
   992→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
   993→        <button
   994→          onClick={() => setLoaded(true)}
   995→          className="rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-purple-700 transition-colors"
   996→        >
   997→          Load your repos
   998→        </button>
   999→      </div>
  1000→    );
  1001→  }
  1002→
  1003→  if (isLoading) {
  1004→    return (
  1005→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
  1006→        <div className="text-sm text-zinc-400">Loading your repos...</div>
  1007→      </div>
  1008→    );
  1009→  }
  1010→
  1011→  if (error) {
  1012→    return (
  1013→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
  1014→        <div className="text-sm text-red-400">Failed to load repos. Try again later.</div>
  1015→      </div>
  1016→    );
  1017→  }
  1018→
  1019→  if (step === "pick") {
  1020→    return <RepoPicker repos={repos} selected={selectedRepos} onToggle={toggleRepo} onScan={handleScan} />;
  1021→  }
  1022→
  1023→  if (step === "scanning") {
  1024→    return <ScanProgress scans={scans} />;
  1025→  }
  1026→
  1027→  if (step === "results") {
  1028→    return <ScanResults scans={scans.filter((s) => s.status === "completed")} onApply={handleApply} />;
  1029→  }
  1030→
  1031→  return (
  1032→    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-12 gap-3">
  1033→      <div className="text-sm text-green-400">
  1034→        Successfully started tracking {appliedCount} dependencies.
  1035→      </div>
  1036→      <button
  1037→        onClick={() => {
  1038→          setStep("pick");
  1039→          setScans([]);
  1040→          setSelectedRepos(new Set());
  1041→        }}
  1042→        className="text-xs text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200 underline"
  1043→      >
  1044→        Scan more repos
  1045→      </button>
  1046→    </div>
  1047→  );
  1048→}
  1049→
  1050→function RepoPicker({
  1051→  repos,
  1052→  selected,
  1053→  onToggle,
  1054→  onScan,
  1055→}: {
  1056→  repos: RepoItem[];
  1057→  selected: Set<string>;
  1058→  onToggle: (name: string) => void;
  1059→  onScan: () => void;
  1060→}) {
  1061→  if (repos.length === 0) {
  1062→    return (
  1063→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
  1064→        <div className="text-sm text-zinc-400">No public repos found.</div>
  1065→      </div>
  1066→    );
  1067→  }
  1068→
  1069→  return (
  1070→    <div>
  1071→      <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400 mb-3">Select repos to scan for dependencies:</p>
  1072→      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto">
  1073→        {repos.map((repo) => (
  1074→          <label
  1075→            key={repo.full_name}
  1076→            className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800 p-3 cursor-pointer hover:border-zinc-600 transition-colors"
  1077→          >
  1078→            <input
  1079→              type="checkbox"
  1080→              checked={selected.has(repo.full_name)}
  1081→              onChange={() => onToggle(repo.full_name)}
  1082→              className="accent-purple-600"
  1083→            />
  1084→            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
  1085→              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-200 truncate">{repo.full_name}</div>
  1086→              <div className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
  1087→                {repo.pushed_at && `Pushed ${new Date(repo.pushed_at).toLocaleDateString()}`}
  1088→                {repo.language && ` · ${repo.language}`}
  1089→              </div>
  1090→            </div>
  1091→          </label>
  1092→        ))}
  1093→      </div>
  1094→      <div className="flex justify-end mt-3">
  1095→        <button
  1096→          onClick={onScan}
  1097→          disabled={selected.size === 0}
  1098→          className="rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-purple-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
  1099→        >
  1100→          Scan Selected ({selected.size})
  1101→        </button>
  1102→      </div>
  1103→    </div>
  1104→  );
  1105→}
  1106→
  1107→function ScanProgress({ scans }: { scans: ScanState[] }) {
  1108→  return (
  1109→    <div className="space-y-2 py-4">
  1110→      <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400 mb-3">Scanning repositories for dependencies...</p>
  1111→      {scans.map((scan) => (
  1112→        <div key={scan.repoName} className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm">
  1113→          <span className="w-5 text-center">
  1114→            {scan.status === "completed" && "✓"}
  1115→            {scan.status === "failed" && "✗"}
  1116→            {(scan.status === "queued" || scan.status === "processing") && "⏳"}
  1117→          </span>
  1118→          <span className={scan.status === "failed" ? "text-red-400" : "text-zinc-300"}>
  1119→            {scan.repoName}
  1120→          </span>
  1121→          {scan.status === "failed" && scan.error && (
  1122→            <span className="text-xs text-red-400/70">({scan.error})</span>
  1123→          )}
  1124→        </div>
  1125→      ))}
  1126→    </div>
  1127→  );
  1128→}
  1129→
  1130→function ScanResults({
  1131→  scans,
  1132→  onApply,
  1133→}: {
  1134→  scans: ScanState[];
  1135→  onApply: (selections: { scanId: string; items: OnboardSelection[] }[]) => void;
  1136→}) {
  1137→  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<string>>(() => {
  1138→    const all = new Set<string>();
  1139→    scans.forEach((scan) =>
  1140→      scan.results?.forEach((dep) => all.add(`${scan.scanId}:${dep.name}`))
  1141→    );
  1142→    return all;
  1143→  });
  1144→
  1145→  const toggle = (key: string) => {
  1146→    setSelected((prev) => {
  1147→      const next = new Set(prev);
  1148→      if (next.has(key)) next.delete(key);
  1149→      else next.add(key);
  1150→      return next;
  1151→    });
  1152→  };
  1153→
  1154→  const handleApply = () => {
  1155→    const selections = scans.map((scan) => ({
  1156→      scanId: scan.scanId,
  1157→      items: (scan.results ?? [])
  1158→        .filter((dep) => selected.has(`${scan.scanId}:${dep.name}`))
  1159→        .map((dep) => ({
  1160→          dep_name: dep.name,
  1161→          upstream_repo: dep.upstream_repo,
  1162→          provider: dep.provider,
  1163→          new_project_name: dep.upstream_repo || dep.name,
  1164→        })),
  1165→    }));
  1166→    onApply(selections);
  1167→  };
  1168→
  1169→  const totalSelected = selected.size;
  1170→
  1171→  if (scans.length === 0 || scans.every((s) => !s.results?.length)) {
  1172→    return (
  1173→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
  1174→        <div className="text-sm text-zinc-400">No dependencies found in the selected repos.</div>
  1175→      </div>
  1176→    );
  1177→  }
  1178→
  1179→  return (
  1180→    <div>
  1181→      {scans.map((scan) => {
  1182→        if (!scan.results?.length) return null;
  1183→        return (
  1184→          <div key={scan.scanId} className="mb-4">
  1185→            <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-300 mb-2">
  1186→              From {scan.repoName}
  1187→            </h4>
  1188→            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
  1189→              {scan.results.map((dep) => {
  1190→                const key = `${scan.scanId}:${dep.name}`;
  1191→                return (
  1192→                  <label
  1193→                    key={key}
  1194→                    className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800/50 px-3 py-2 cursor-pointer text-sm"
  1195→                  >
  1196→                    <input
  1197→                      type="checkbox"
  1198→                      checked={selected.has(key)}
  1199→                      onChange={() => toggle(key)}
  1200→                      className="accent-purple-600"
  1201→                    />
  1202→                    <span className="text-zinc-200">{dep.name}</span>
  1203→                    {dep.version && (
  1204→                      <span className="text-xs text-zinc-500">{dep.version}</span>
  1205→                    )}
  1206→                    <span className="ml-auto text-xs text-zinc-500">{dep.ecosystem}</span>
  1207→                  </label>
  1208→                );
  1209→              })}
  1210→            </div>
  1211→          </div>
  1212→        );
  1213→      })}
  1214→      <div className="flex justify-end mt-3">
  1215→        <button
  1216→          onClick={handleApply}
  1217→          disabled={totalSelected === 0}
  1218→          className="rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-purple-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
  1219→        >
  1220→          Track Selected ({totalSelected})
  1221→        </button>
  1222→      </div>
  1223→    </div>
  1224→  );
  1225→}

     1→# Implementer Subagent Prompt Template
     2→
     3→Use this template when dispatching an implementer subagent.
     4→
     5→```
     6→Task tool (general-purpose):
     7→  description: "Implement Task N: [task name]"
     8→  prompt: |
     9→    You are implementing Task N: [task name]
    10→
    11→    ## Task Description
    12→
    13→    [FULL TEXT of task from plan - paste it here, don't make subagent read file]
    14→
    15→    ## Context
    16→
    17→    [Scene-setting: where this fits, dependencies, architectural context]
    18→
    19→    ## Before You Begin
    20→
    21→    If you have questions about:
    22→    - The requirements or acceptance criteria
    23→    - The approach or implementation strategy
    24→    - Dependencies or assumptions
    25→    - Anything unclear in the task description
    26→
    27→    **Ask them now.** Raise any concerns before starting work.
    28→
    29→    ## Your Job
    30→
    31→    Once you're clear on requirements:
    32→    1. Implement exactly what the task specifies
    33→    2. Write tests (following TDD if task says to)
    34→    3. Verify implementation works
    35→    4. Commit your work
    36→    5. Self-review (see below)
    37→    6. Report back
    38→
    39→    Work from: [directory]
    40→
    41→    **While you work:** If you encounter something unexpected or unclear, **ask questions**.
    42→    It's always OK to pause and clarify. Don't guess or make assumptions.
    43→
    44→    ## Code Organization
    45→
    46→    You reason best about code you can hold in context at once, and your edits are more
    47→    reliable when files are focused. Keep this in mind:
    48→    - Follow the file structure defined in the plan
    49→    - Each file should have one clear responsibility with a well-defined interface
    50→    - If a file you're creating is growing beyond the plan's intent, stop and report
    51→      it as DONE_WITH_CONCERNS — don't split files on your own without plan guidance
    52→    - If an existing file you're modifying is already large or tangled, work carefully
    53→      and note it as a concern in your report
    54→    - In existing codebases, follow established patterns. Improve code you're touching
    55→      the way a good developer would, but don't restructure things outside your task.
    56→
    57→    ## When You're in Over Your Head
    58→
    59→    It is always OK to stop and say "this is too hard for me." Bad work is worse than
    60→    no work. You will not be penalized for escalating.
    61→
    62→    **STOP and escalate when:**
    63→    - The task requires architectural decisions with multiple valid approaches
    64→    - You need to understand code beyond what was provided and can't find clarity
    65→    - You feel uncertain about whether your approach is correct
    66→    - The task involves restructuring existing code in ways the plan didn't anticipate
    67→    - You've been reading file after file trying to understand the system without progress
    68→
    69→    **How to escalate:** Report back with status BLOCKED or NEEDS_CONTEXT. Describe
    70→    specifically what you're stuck on, what you've tried, and what kind of help you need.
    71→    The controller can provide more context, re-dispatch with a more capable model,
    72→    or break the task into smaller pieces.
    73→
    74→    ## Before Reporting Back: Self-Review
    75→
    76→    Review your work with fresh eyes. Ask yourself:
    77→
    78→    **Completeness:**
    79→    - Did I fully implement everything in the spec?
    80→    - Did I miss any requirements?
    81→    - Are there edge cases I didn't handle?
    82→
    83→    **Quality:**
    84→    - Is this my best work?
    85→    - Are names clear and accurate (match what things do, not how they work)?
    86→    - Is the code clean and maintainable?
    87→
    88→    **Discipline:**
    89→    - Did I avoid overbuilding (YAGNI)?
    90→    - Did I only build what was requested?
    91→    - Did I follow existing patterns in the codebase?
    92→
    93→    **Testing:**
    94→    - Do tests actually verify behavior (not just mock behavior)?
    95→    - Did I follow TDD if required?
    96→    - Are tests comprehensive?
    97→
    98→    If you find issues during self-review, fix them now before reporting.
    99→
   100→    ## Report Format
   101→
   102→    When done, report:
   103→    - **Status:** DONE | DONE_WITH_CONCERNS | BLOCKED | NEEDS_CONTEXT
   104→    - What you implemented (or what you attempted, if blocked)
   105→    - What you tested and test results
   106→    - Files changed
   107→    - Self-review findings (if any)
   108→    - Any issues or concerns
   109→
   110→    Use DONE_WITH_CONCERNS if you completed the work but have doubts about correctness.
   111→    Use BLOCKED if you cannot complete the task. Use NEEDS_CONTEXT if you need
   112→    information that wasn't provided. Never silently produce work you're unsure about.

{
  "description": "Implement Task 1: Store layer",
  "prompt": "You are implementing Task 1: Add ListAllSourceRepos to store layer\n\n## Task Description\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: `internal/api/sources.go:15-22` (SourcesStore interface)\n- Modify: `internal/api/pgstore.go` (add new method after line ~205)\n- Modify: `internal/api/sources_test.go` (add to mock)\n- Modify: `internal/models/source.go` (add SourceRepo struct)\n\n**Step 1: Add method to SourcesStore interface**\n\nIn `internal/api/sources.go`, add to the `SourcesStore` interface:\n\n```go\nListAllSourceRepos(ctx context.Context) ([]models.SourceRepo, error)\n```\n\nAnd in `internal/models/source.go`, add:\n\n```go\ntype SourceRepo struct {\n\tProvider   string `json:\"provider\"`\n\tRepository string `json:\"repository\"`\n}\n```\n\n**Step 2: Implement in PgStore**\n\nIn `internal/api/pgstore.go`, add after the `UpdateSourcePollStatus` method:\n\n```go\nfunc (s *PgStore) ListAllSourceRepos(ctx context.Context) ([]models.SourceRepo, error) {\n\trows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx, `SELECT DISTINCT provider, repository FROM sources WHERE enabled = true`)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"list all source repos: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\tvar repos []models.SourceRepo\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar r models.SourceRepo\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&r.Provider, &r.Repository); ### `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/pgstore.go`
Added the implementation immediately after `UpdateSourcePollStatus` (before the `// --- ReleasesStore ---` comment):
```go
func (s *PgStore) ListAllSourceRepos(ctx context.Context) ([]models.SourceRepo, error) {
    rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx, `SELECT DISTINCT provider, repository FROM sources WHERE enabled = true`)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("list all source repos: %w", err)
    }
    defer rows.Close()
    var repos []models.SourceRepo
    for rows.Next() {
        var r models.SourceRepo
        if err := rows.Scan(&r.Provider, &r.Repository); err != nil {
            return nil, fmt.Errorf("scan source repo: %w", err)
        }
        repos = append(repos, r)
    }
    return repos, rows.Err()
}
```

### `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/sources_test.go`
Added the mock method to `mockSourcesStore` after `UpdateSourcePollStatus`:
```go
func (m *mockSourcesStore) ListAllSourceRepos(_ context.Context) ([]models.SourceRepo, error) {
    return nil, nil
}
```

No other mocks of `SourcesStore` exist in the codebase — `mockSourcesStore` in `sources_test.go` is the only one. The `PgStore` struct correctly uses `s.pool` (a `*pgxpool.Pool`) throughout.

[];\n\n  const handleTrack = useCallback(async (item: SuggestionItem) => {\n    const key = item.full_name;\n    setTrackingIds((prev) => new Set(prev).add(key));\n    try {\n      const projRes = await projects.create({\n        name: item.full_name,\n        description: item.description,\n      });\n      const project = projRes.data;\n      const sourceRes = await sources.create(project.id, {\n        provider: \"github\",\n        repository: item.full_name,\n        poll_interval_seconds: 86400,\n        enabled: true,\n      });\n      await sources.poll(sourceRes.data.id);\n      await mutate(\"suggestions-stars\");\n      await mutate(\"projects-list\");\n      await mutate(\"projects-for-dashboard\");\n    } catch (err: unknown) {\n      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : \"Failed to track\";\n      alert(msg);\n    } finally {\n      setTrackingIds((prev) => {\n        const next = new Set(prev);\n        next.delete(key);\n        return next;\n      });\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  if (!loaded) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-12\">\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setLoaded(true)}\n          className=\"rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-purple-700 transition-colors\"\n        >\n          Load your starred repos\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (isLoading) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-12\">\n        <div className=\"text-sm text-zinc-400\">Loading your starred repos...</div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (error) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-12\">\n        <div className=\"text-sm text-red-400\">Failed to load stars. (\n              <span className=\"shrink-0 text-xs text-green-400\">✓ Tracked</span>\n            ) : (\n              <button\n                onClick={() => handleTrack(item)}\n                disabled={trackingIds.has(item.full_name)}\n                className=\"shrink-0 rounded bg-purple-600 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-white hover:bg-purple-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors\"\n              >\n                {trackingIds.has(item.full_name) ? \"Tracking...\" : \"Track\"}\n              </button>\n            )}\n          </div>\n          {item.description && (\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-zinc-400 mb-2 line-clamp-2\">\n              {item.description}\n            </p>\n          )}\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-zinc-500\">\n            <span>⭐ {item.stars.toLocaleString()}</span>\n            {item.language && <span>● {item.language}</span>}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      ))}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n```\n\n## Context\n\nTask 4 (types and API client) is already complete.  <label\n            key={repo.full_name}\n            className=\"flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800 p-3 cursor-pointer hover:border-zinc-600 transition-colors\"\n The task is complete. Here is a summary of what was done.

## Summary

### File created
`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/dashboard/deps-tab.tsx` — 343 lines

### Type verification findings

Before writing the component, I confirmed the following from the actual source files:

| Type / Field | Actual definition |
|---|---|
| `OnboardApplyResult.created_sources` | `Source[]` — array, so `.length` is correct |
| `OnboardApplyResult.created_projects` | `Project[]` |
| `OnboardApplyResult.skipped` | `string[]` |
| `ScannedDependency.version` | `string` (non-optional, always present) |
| `onboard.scan()` return | `Promise<ApiResponse<OnboardScan>>` — accessed as `scanRes.data.id` |
| `onboard.getScan()` return | `Promise<ApiResponse<OnboardScan>>` — accessed as `pollRes.data` |
| `RepoItem` | Has `full_name`, `language?`, `pushed_at`, `url` |

All fields in the template matched the actual types — no adaptation was needed.

### Component structure

The `DepsTab` export drives a 4-step flow via a `Step` type union:

1. **`pick`** — `RepoPicker` renders a lazy-loaded checkbox list of the user's GitHub repos (fetched via `suggestions.repos()` only after clicking "Load your repos").
2. **`scanning`** — `ScanProgress` shows live per-repo status as scans are submitted and polled in batches of 5, with 2-second polling intervals.
3. **`results`** — `ScanResults` renders all discovered dependencies with select-all-by-default checkboxes grouped by repo; calls `onboard.apply()` for each scan.
4. **`applied`** — Shows the count of newly tracked sources and a "Scan more repos" reset link.

### Verification
`npx tsc --noEmit` produced zero errors.

---

## Task 2: Backend Suggestions Handler -- Complete

### Files Created

**`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/suggestions.go`** -- The main handler with:

- **`SuggestionsHandler`** struct holding an HTTP client, store interface, GitHub token, base URL, and a per-user cache (`map[string]suggestionsCache`) protected by a mutex.
- **`NewSuggestionsHandler`** constructor that falls back to `GITHUB_TOKEN` env var and `https://api.github.com` defaults.
- **`Stars()`** handler: Fetches up to 4 pages (30 items each) of a user's GitHub starred repos, caches the raw JSON, cross-references against tracked repos via `SuggestionsSourceStore.ListAllSourceRepos`, and returns `[]SuggestionItem` with a `tracked` boolean.
- **`Repos()`** handler: Fetches the user's own repos sorted by push date (up to 100), caches the result, and returns `[]RepoItem`.
- **Cache layer**: `getCached`/`setCache` with a 1-hour TTL, keyed by `"stars:<login>"` or `"repos:<login>"`.
- **Graceful fallback**: Returns an empty array for unauthenticated users, users without a GitHub login, or the "dev" user (NO_AUTH mode).

**`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/suggestions_test.go`** -- Four tests:

| Test | What it verifies |
|---|---|
| `TestSuggestionsStars` | Full stars flow with mock GitHub server; checks tracked/untracked flags |
| `TestSuggestionsStarsNoUser` | Returns empty array when no user in context |
| `TestSuggestionsRepos` | Full repos flow with mock GitHub server |
| `TestSuggestionsStarsCaching` | Three identical requests produce only 1 GitHub API call |

### Verification

- `go test -v -run TestSuggestions ./internal/api/...` -- **all 4 tests PASS**
- `go vet ./internal/api/...` -- clean, no issues

Implementation complete. Here's a summary:

## What was built

**Personalized GitHub Suggestions** — replaces the trending discovery carousel for GitHub-authenticated users with two tabs:

### Backend (Go)
- **`internal/api/suggestions.go`** — `SuggestionsHandler` with `Stars()` and `Repos()` endpoints that proxy the public GitHub API using the server-side `GITHUB_TOKEN`
- **`GET /api/v1/suggestions/stars`** — fetches user's starred repos (up to 120), marks already-tracked ones with `tracked: true`, caches 1hr per user
- **`GET /api/v1/suggestions/repos`** — fetches user's public repos sorted by last push (up to 100), cached 1hr
- **`ListAllSourceRepos`** — new store method for tracked-repo filtering
- 4 unit tests covering stars, repos, no-user fallback, and caching

### Frontend (Next.js/React)
- **`SuggestionsSection`** — tabbed container ("Your Stars" | "Your Dependencies")
- **`StarsTab`** — on-demand load, grid of starred repos with one-click Track buttons
- **`DepsTab`** — multi-step: pick repos → scan for deps (reuses onboard pipeline) → review results → track selected
- Dashboard conditionally renders suggestions vs. trending carousel based on `github_login`

### 10 commits, all tests passing
- `go test ./...` — all green
- `go build` — clean
- `npx tsc --noEmit` — no errors

    10→
    11→function formatStars(count: number): string {
    12→  if (count >= 1000) {
    13→    const k = count / 1000;
    14→    return k >= 10 ? `${Math.round(k)}k` : `${Math.round(k * 10) / 10}k`;
    15→  }
    16→  return String(count);
    17→}
    18→
    19→export function DiscoverySection() {
    20→  const [trackingIds, setTrackingIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
    21→  const [paused, setPaused] = useState(false);
    22→
    23→  const { data: githubData, isLoading } = useSWR(
    24→    "discover-github",
    25→    () => discover.github(),
    26→    { revalidateOnFocus: false },
    27→  );
    28→
    29→  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR(
    30→    "projects-list",
    31→    () => projects.list(1, 200),
    32→    { revalidateOnFocus: false },
    33→  );
    34→
    35→  const trackedNames = useMemo(
    36→    () =>
    37→      new Set(
    38→        (projectsData?.data ?? []).flatMap((p) => [
    39→          p.name,
    40→          p.name.split("/").pop()!,
    41→        ]),
    42→      ),
    43→    [projectsData],
    44→  );
    45→
    46→  const allItems = useMemo(() => githubData?.data ?? [], [githubData]);
    47→
    48→  const isTracked = useCallback(

    53→
    54→  const handleTrack = useCallback(
    55→    async (item: DiscoverItem) => {
    56→      const key = item.full_name;
    57→      setTrackingIds((prev) => new Set(prev).add(key));
    58→      try {
    59→        const projRes = await projects.create({
    60→          name: item.full_name,

    69→          poll_interval_seconds: 86400,

    71→        });
    72→
    73→        await sources.poll(sourceRes.data.id);
    74→        await mutate("projects-list");
    75→        await mutate("projects-for-dashboard");
    76→      } catch (err: unknown) {
    77→        const msg =
    78→          err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to track project";
    79→        alert(msg);
    80→      } finally {
    81→        setTrackingIds((prev) => {
    82→          const next = new Set(prev);
    83→          next.delete(key);
    84→          return next;
    85→        });
    86→      }
    87→    },
    88→    [],
    89→  );
    90→
