   107→          </span>
   108→          <div className="flex gap-3 overflow-hidden">
   109→            {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => (
   110→              <div
   111→                key={i}
   112→                className="h-8 w-48 flex-shrink-0 animate-pulse rounded-full"
   113→                style={{ backgroundColor: "#f0f0ed" }}
   114→              />
   115→            ))}
   116→          </div>
   117→        </div>
   118→      </div>
   119→    );
   120→  }
   121→
   122→  if (allItems.length === 0) return null;
   123→
   124→  return (
   125→    <div
   126→      className="rounded-lg bg-white"
   127→      style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", overflow: "hidden" }}
   128→    >
   129→      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 px-5 py-3">
   130→        {/* Label */}
   131→        <span
   132→          className="flex-shrink-0"
   133→          style={{
   134→            fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   135→            fontSize: "14px",
   136→            fontWeight: 600,
   137→            color: "#111113",
   138→          }}
   139→        >
   140→          Trending
   141→        </span>
   142→
   143→        {/* Marquee container */}
   144→        <div
   145→          className="relative flex-1 overflow-hidden"
   146→          onMouseEnter={() => setPaused(true)}
   147→          onMouseLeave={() => setPaused(false)}
   148→        >
   149→          {/* Fade edges */}
   150→          <div
   151→            className="pointer-events-none absolute left-0 top-0 z-10 h-full w-8"
   152→            style={{
   153→              background:
   154→                "linear-gradient(to right, white, transparent)",
   155→            }}
   156→          />
   157→          <div
   158→            className="pointer-events-none absolute right-0 top-0 z-10 h-full w-8"
   159→            style={{
   160→              background:
   161→                "linear-gradient(to left, white, transparent)",
   162→            }}
   163→          />
   164→
   165→          {/* Scrolling track — duplicated for seamless loop */}
   166→          <div
   167→            className="flex gap-2.5"
   168→            style={{
   169→              animationName: "marquee",
   170→              animationDuration: `${Math.max(allItems.length * 6, 60)}s`,
   171→              animationTimingFunction: "linear",
   172→              animationIterationCount: "infinite",
   173→              animationPlayState: paused ? "paused" : "running",
   174→              width: "max-content",
   175→            }}
   176→          >
   177→            {[...allItems, ...allItems].map((item, idx) => {
   178→              const tracked = isTracked(item);
   179→              const tracking = trackingIds.has(item.full_name);
   180→
   181→              return (
   182→                <MarqueeChip
   183→                  key={`${item.full_name}-${idx}`}
   184→                  item={item}
   185→                  tracked={tracked}
   186→                  tracking={tracking}
   187→                  onTrack={handleTrack}
   188→                />
   189→              );
   190→            })}
   191→          </div>
   192→        </div>
   193→      </div>
   194→
   195→      {/* Keyframes injected via style tag */}
   196→      <style>{`
   197→        @keyframes marquee {
   198→          0% { transform: translateX(0); }
   199→          100% { transform: translateX(-50%); }
   200→        }
   201→      `}</style>
   202→    </div>
   203→  );
   204→}
   205→
   206→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
   207→
   208→function MarqueeChip({
   209→  item,
   210→  tracked,
   211→  tracking,
   212→  onTrack,
   213→}: {
   214→  item: DiscoverItem;
   215→  tracked: boolean;
   216→  tracking: boolean;
   217→  onTrack: (item: DiscoverItem) => void;
   218→}) {
   219→  const ProviderIcon =
   220→    item.provider === "github" ? FaGithub : FaDocker;
   221→
   222→  if (tracked) {
   223→    return (
   224→      <span
   225→        className="inline-flex flex-shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-3 py-1.5"
   226→        style={{
   227→          fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   228→          fontSize: "12px",
   229→          color: "#16a34a",
   230→          backgroundColor: "#f0fdf4",
   231→          border: "1px solid #bbf7d0",
   232→          whiteSpace: "nowrap",
   233→        }}
   234→      >
   235→        <ProviderIcon className="h-3 w-3" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }} />
   236→        {item.full_name}
   237→        <Check className="h-3 w-3" />
   238→      </span>
   239→    );
   240→  }
   241→
   242→  return (
   243→    <button
   244→      onClick={() => onTrack(item)}
   245→      disabled={tracking}
   246→      className="inline-flex flex-shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 transition-all hover:shadow-sm disabled:opacity-70"
   247→      style={{
   248→        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   249→        fontSize: "12px",
   250→        color: "#111113",
   251→        backgroundColor: "#fafaf9",
   252→        border: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   253→        whiteSpace: "nowrap",
   254→        cursor: tracking ? "wait" : "pointer",
   255→      }}
   256→    >
   257→      <ProviderIcon className="h-3 w-3" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }} />
   258→      <span>{item.full_name}</span>
   259→      <Star className="h-3 w-3" style={{ color: "#e8601a" }} />
   260→      <span style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontSize: "11px" }}>
   261→        {formatStars(item.stars)}
   262→      </span>
   263→      {tracking ? (
   264→        <Loader2
   265→          className="h-3 w-3 animate-spin"
   266→          style={{ color: "#e8601a" }}
   267→        />
   268→      ) : (
   269→        <Plus className="h-3 w-3" style={{ color: "#e8601a" }} />
   270→      )}
   271→    </button>
   272→  );
   273→}
   274→

Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive view of the styling patterns. Let me compile the final report.

## Changelogue Web App Styling Patterns

### 1. **CSS Theme: Light Mode (Primary)**
The app uses a **light theme** with carefully curated neutral tones and a vibrant accent color. Dark mode support exists via CSS custom properties but the primary design target is light.

### 2. **Core Color Tokens** (from `globals.css`)

| Token | Value | Purpose |
|-------|-------|---------|
| **Primary** | `#e8601a` (burnt orange) | Primary CTA, active nav, focus ring |
| **Primary Foreground** | `#ffffff` | Text on primary buttons |
| **Background** | `#fafaf9` (off-white) | Page background |
| **Foreground** | `#111113` (near-black) | Primary text color |
| **Surface** | `#ffffff` | Card/container backgrounds |
| **Card Background** | `#ffffff` | Cards, popovers |
| **Card Foreground** | `#111113` | Text in cards |
| **Sidebar Background** | `#16181c` (dark charcoal) | Navigation sidebar |
| **Sidebar Text** | `#9ca3af` (gray) | Inactive nav items, sidebar icons |
| **Border** | `#e8e8e5` (light gray) | Borders, dividers, input borders |
| **Input** | `#e8e8e5` | Input field background |
| **Muted Background** | `#f3f3f1` (very light beige) | Code blocks, stats cards, accents |
| **Muted Foreground** | `#6b7280` | Secondary/helper text |
| **Text Muted** | `#9ca3af` | Tertiary text, hints |
| **Destructive** | `#dc2626` (red) | Error states, delete actions |
| **Beacon Accent** | `#e8601a` | Same as primary, custom extension |

**Status Colors:**
- Completed: `#16a34a` (green)
- Running: `#2563eb` (blue)
- Pending: `#d97706` (amber)
- Failed: `#dc2626` (red)

### 3. **Typography**

**Font Families:**
- **Display/Headings:** Fraunces (from Google Fonts) — Elegant serif with variable axes (SOFT, WONK)
- **Body/UI:** DM Sans (from Google Fonts) — Clean, modern sans-serif
- **Code/Monospace:** JetBrains Mono — From `@fontsource/jetbrains-mono/400.css`

**Font Sizes:**
- Stats cards value: `32px` (Fraunces, bold)
- Stats label: `12px` uppercase (DM Sans, tracking)
- Body: `text-sm` base in Tailwind

### 4. **Border & Radius**

| Token | Value |
|-------|-------|
| Base Radius | `0.375rem` (6px) |
| `--radius-sm` | `2px` |
| `--radius-md` | `4px` |
| `--radius-lg` | `6px` (base) |
| `--radius-xl` | `10px` |
| Cards | `rounded-xl` (10px) with `1px` border |
| Buttons | `rounded-md` (6px) |
| Badges | `rounded-full` |

### 5. **Button Styles (CVA Variants)**

**Variants:**
- **default:** Orange primary (`bg-primary text-primary-foreground`) with hover opacity 90%
- **destructive:** Red with white text (`#dc2626`)
- **outline:** Bordered with accent hover
- **secondary:** Light gray background (`#f3f3f1`)
- **ghost:** No background, accent on hover
- **link:** Text link with underline

**Sizes:**
- `default`: h-9 (36px)
- `sm`: h-8 (32px)
- `xs`: h-6 (24px)
- `lg`: h-10 (40px)
- `icon`, `icon-sm`, `icon-md`, `icon-lg` for square buttons

### 6. **Card Style** (from `card.tsx`)

```
Classes: "bg-card text-card-foreground flex flex-col gap-6 rounded-xl border py-6 shadow-sm"
- Background: white (#ffffff)
- Border: 1px solid #e8e8e5
- Padding: py-6 (24px), px-6 (24px) per section
- Border radius: rounded-xl (10px)
- Shadow: shadow-sm (subtle elevation)
- Gap between sections: gap-6 (24px)
```

**Card Subcomponents:**
- **CardHeader:** @container/card-header with grid layout, gap-2
- **CardTitle:** font-semibold
- **CardDescription:** text-sm, color: #6b7280
- **CardContent:** px-6 padding
- **CardFooter:** flex items-center, px-6, with border-top support

### 7. **Icon Libraries**

1. **lucide-react** (`^0.575.0`) — Primary icon library
   - Used for UI icons (LayoutDashboard, FolderKanban, Package, Bell, LogOut, TrendingUp, etc.)
   - 4px default size with CSS class: `h-4 w-4`

2. **react-icons** (`^5.5.0`) — Secondary icon library (installed but less visible in core components)

### 8. **Input & Form Elements**

```tailwind
Classes: "h-9 w-full rounded-md border bg-transparent px-3 py-1 text-base"
- Height: 36px
- Border: 1px solid #e8e8e5
- Background: transparent (then styled via input color)
- Border radius: rounded-md (6px)
- Focus: border-ring (#e8601a) with ring-ring/50
```

### 9. **Special Design Elements**

**Sidebar Navigation:**
- Active state: `border-left: 3px solid #e8601a`, `background: rgba(255,255,255,0.06)`
- Inactive text: `#9ca3af`, hover → white
- Text: 13px

**Stats Cards (Custom Styling):**
- Background: white
- Border: 1px solid #e8e8e5
- Padding: px-5 py-4
- Icon color: `#b0b0a8` (muted)
- Value font: Fraunces 32px, `#111113`
- Label: 12px, `#6b7280`, uppercase tracking

**Release Notes Content** (custom `.release-notes-content` class):
- Headings: font-weight 600, color `#111113`
- Code blocks: background `#f3f3f1`, font JetBrains Mono
- Links: color `#2563eb`, underlined
- Blockquotes: `3px solid #e8e8e5` border-left, text `#6b7280`

### 10. **Utility Classes & Animations**

**Custom CSS Variables System:**
```css
--color-background: var(--background)
--color-foreground: var(--foreground)
--color-primary: var(--primary)
--color-border: var(--border)
--color-sidebar: var(--sidebar-bg)
--color-beacon-accent: var(--beacon-accent)
```

**Motion:**
- `.fade-in` animation: 150ms ease-in-out from opacity 0→1
- Transitions: `transition-all`, `transition-colors`, `duration-200`, `duration-150`

**CSS Framework:**
- Tailwind CSS 4 with PostCSS
- shadcn/ui components (imported via `@import "shadcn/tailwind.css"`)
- class-variance-authority (CVA) for variant-based styling
- tailwind-merge for class merging

---

## Key Takeaway: Design System
Changelogue uses a **light, minimalist design** centered on:
- **Neutral palette** (white, beige, grays) with **single accent color** (#e8601a)
- **Two-tier typography**: Fraunces for display, DM Sans for UI
- **Subtle shadows and borders** (shadow-sm, 1px borders in light gray)
- **Consistent spacing** via Tailwind gap/padding system
- **shadcn/ui + CVA** for composable, variant-driven components
- **lucide-react** for all decorative and functional icons

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { useState, useCallback } from "react";
     4→import useSWR, { mutate } from "swr";
     5→import { suggestions, projects, sources } from "@/lib/api/client";
     6→import type { SuggestionItem } from "@/lib/api/types";
     7→
     8→export function StarsTab() {
     9→  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
    10→  const [trackingIds, setTrackingIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
    11→
    12→  const { data, isLoading, error } = useSWR(
    13→    loaded ? "suggestions-stars" : null,
    14→    () => suggestions.stars(),
    15→    { revalidateOnFocus: false }
    16→  );
    17→
    18→  const items = data?.data ?? [];
    19→
    20→  const handleTrack = useCallback(async (item: SuggestionItem) => {
    21→    const key = item.full_name;
    22→    setTrackingIds((prev) => new Set(prev).add(key));
    23→    try {
    24→      const projRes = await projects.create({
    25→        name: item.full_name,
    26→        description: item.description,
    27→      });
    28→      const project = projRes.data;
    29→      const sourceRes = await sources.create(project.id, {
    30→        provider: "github",
    31→        repository: item.full_name,
    32→        poll_interval_seconds: 86400,
    33→        enabled: true,
    34→      });
    35→      await sources.poll(sourceRes.data.id);
    36→      await mutate("suggestions-stars");
    37→      await mutate("projects-list");
    38→      await mutate("projects-for-dashboard");
    39→    } catch (err: unknown) {
    40→      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to track";
    41→      alert(msg);
    42→    } finally {
    43→      setTrackingIds((prev) => {
    44→        const next = new Set(prev);
    45→        next.delete(key);
    46→        return next;
    47→      });
    48→    }
    49→  }, []);
    50→
    51→  if (!loaded) {
    52→    return (
    53→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
    54→        <button
    55→          onClick={() => setLoaded(true)}
    56→          className="rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-purple-700 transition-colors"
    57→        >
    58→          Load your starred repos
    59→        </button>
    60→      </div>
    61→    );
    62→  }
    63→
    64→  if (isLoading) {
    65→    return (
    66→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
    67→        <div className="text-sm text-zinc-400">Loading your starred repos...</div>
    68→      </div>
    69→    );
    70→  }
    71→
    72→  if (error) {
    73→    return (
    74→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
    75→        <div className="text-sm text-red-400">Failed to load stars. Try again later.</div>
    76→      </div>
    77→    );
    78→  }
    79→
    80→  if (items.length === 0) {
    81→    return (
    82→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
    83→        <div className="text-sm text-zinc-400">
    84→          You haven&apos;t starred any public repos on GitHub yet.
    85→        </div>
    86→      </div>
    87→    );
    88→  }
    89→
    90→  return (
    91→    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
    92→      {items.map((item) => (
    93→        <div
    94→          key={item.full_name}
    95→          className={`rounded-lg border p-3 ${
    96→            item.tracked
    97→              ? "border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800/50 opacity-60"
    98→              : "border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800"
    99→          }`}
   100→        >
   101→          <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 mb-2">
   102→            <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-200 truncate">
   103→              {item.full_name}
   104→            </span>
   105→            {item.tracked ? (
   106→              <span className="shrink-0 text-xs text-green-400">✓ Tracked</span>
   107→            ) : (
   108→              <button
   109→                onClick={() => handleTrack(item)}
   110→                disabled={trackingIds.has(item.full_name)}
   111→                className="shrink-0 rounded bg-purple-600 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-white hover:bg-purple-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
   112→              >
   113→                {trackingIds.has(item.full_name) ? "Tracking..." : "Track"}
   114→              </button>
   115→            )}
   116→          </div>
   117→          {item.description && (
   118→            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mb-2 line-clamp-2">
   119→              {item.description}
   120→            </p>
   121→          )}
   122→          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-zinc-500">
   123→            <span>⭐ {item.stars.toLocaleString()}</span>
   124→            {item.language && <span>● {item.language}</span>}
   125→          </div>
   126→        </div>
   127→      ))}
   128→    </div>
   129→  );
   130→}
   131→

     1→// web/components/dashboard/discovery-section.tsx
     2→"use client";
     3→
     4→import { useState, useCallback, useMemo } from "react";
     5→import useSWR, { mutate } from "swr";
     6→import { Star, Loader2, Check, Plus } from "lucide-react";
     7→import { FaGithub, FaDocker } from "react-icons/fa";
     8→import { discover, projects, sources } from "@/lib/api/client";
     9→import type { DiscoverItem } from "@/lib/api/types";
    10→
    11→function formatStars(count: number): string {
    12→  if (count >= 1000) {
    13→    const k = count / 1000;
    14→    return k >= 10 ? `${Math.round(k)}k` : `${Math.round(k * 10) / 10}k`;
    15→  }
    16→  return String(count);
    17→}
    18→
    19→export function DiscoverySection() {
    20→  const [trackingIds, setTrackingIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
    21→  const [paused, setPaused] = useState(false);
    22→
    23→  const { data: githubData, isLoading } = useSWR(
    24→    "discover-github",
    25→    () => discover.github(),
    26→    { revalidateOnFocus: false },
    27→  );
    28→
    29→  const { data: projectsData } = useSWR(
    30→    "projects-list",
    31→    () => projects.list(1, 200),
    32→    { revalidateOnFocus: false },
    33→  );
    34→
    35→  const trackedNames = useMemo(
    36→    () =>
    37→      new Set(
    38→        (projectsData?.data ?? []).flatMap((p) => [
    39→          p.name,
    40→          p.name.split("/").pop()!,
    41→        ]),
    42→      ),
    43→    [projectsData],
    44→  );
    45→
    46→  const allItems = useMemo(() => githubData?.data ?? [], [githubData]);
    47→
    48→  const isTracked = useCallback(
    49→    (item: DiscoverItem) =>
    50→      trackedNames.has(item.full_name) || trackedNames.has(item.name),
    51→    [trackedNames],
    52→  );
    53→
    54→  const handleTrack = useCallback(
    55→    async (item: DiscoverItem) => {
    56→      const key = item.full_name;
    57→      setTrackingIds((prev) => new Set(prev).add(key));
    58→      try {
    59→        const projRes = await projects.create({
    60→          name: item.full_name,
    61→          description: item.description,
    62→        });
    63→        const project = projRes.data;
    64→
    65→        const provider = item.provider === "github" ? "github" : "dockerhub";
    66→        const sourceRes = await sources.create(project.id, {
    67→          provider,
    68→          repository: item.full_name,
    69→          poll_interval_seconds: 86400,
    70→          enabled: true,
    71→        });
    72→
    73→        await sources.poll(sourceRes.data.id);
    74→        await mutate("projects-list");
    75→        await mutate("projects-for-dashboard");
    76→      } catch (err: unknown) {
    77→        const msg =
    78→          err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to track project";
    79→        alert(msg);
    80→      } finally {
    81→        setTrackingIds((prev) => {
    82→          const next = new Set(prev);
    83→          next.delete(key);
    84→          return next;
    85→        });
    86→      }
    87→    },
    88→    [],
    89→  );
    90→
    91→  if (isLoading) {
    92→    return (
    93→      <div
    94→        className="rounded-lg bg-white px-5 py-4"
    95→        style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }}
    96→      >
    97→        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
    98→          <span
    99→            style={{
   100→              fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   101→              fontSize: "14px",
   102→              fontWeight: 600,
   103→              color: "#111113",
   104→            }}
   105→          >
   106→            Trending
   107→          </span>
   108→          <div className="flex gap-3 overflow-hidden">
   109→            {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => (
   110→              <div
   111→                key={i}
   112→                className="h-8 w-48 flex-shrink-0 animate-pulse rounded-full"
   113→                style={{ backgroundColor: "#f0f0ed" }}
   114→              />
   115→            ))}
   116→          </div>
   117→        </div>
   118→      </div>
   119→    );
   120→  }
   121→
   122→  if (allItems.length === 0) return null;
   123→
   124→  return (
   125→    <div
   126→      className="rounded-lg bg-white"
   127→      style={{ border: "1px solid #e8e8e5", overflow: "hidden" }}
   128→    >
   129→      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 px-5 py-3">
   130→        {/* Label */}
   131→        <span
   132→          className="flex-shrink-0"
   133→          style={{
   134→            fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)",
   135→            fontSize: "14px",
   136→            fontWeight: 600,
   137→            color: "#111113",
   138→          }}
   139→        >
   140→          Trending
   141→        </span>
   142→
   143→        {/* Marquee container */}
   144→        <div
   145→          className="relative flex-1 overflow-hidden"
   146→          onMouseEnter={() => setPaused(true)}
   147→          onMouseLeave={() => setPaused(false)}
   148→        >
   149→          {/* Fade edges */}
   150→          <div
   151→            className="pointer-events-none absolute left-0 top-0 z-10 h-full w-8"
   152→            style={{
   153→              background:
   154→                "linear-gradient(to right, white, transparent)",
   155→            }}
   156→          />
   157→          <div
   158→            className="pointer-events-none absolute right-0 top-0 z-10 h-full w-8"
   159→            style={{
   160→              background:
   161→                "linear-gradient(to left, white, transparent)",
   162→            }}
   163→          />
   164→
   165→          {/* Scrolling track — duplicated for seamless loop */}
   166→          <div
   167→            className="flex gap-2.5"
   168→            style={{
   169→              animationName: "marquee",
   170→              animationDuration: `${Math.max(allItems.length * 6, 60)}s`,
   171→              animationTimingFunction: "linear",
   172→              animationIterationCount: "infinite",
   173→              animationPlayState: paused ? "paused" : "running",
   174→              width: "max-content",
   175→            }}
   176→          >
   177→            {[...allItems, ...allItems].map((item, idx) => {
   178→              const tracked = isTracked(item);
   179→              const tracking = trackingIds.has(item.full_name);
   180→
   181→              return (
   182→                <MarqueeChip
   183→                  key={`${item.full_name}-${idx}`}
   184→                  item={item}
   185→                  tracked={tracked}
   186→                  tracking={tracking}
   187→                  onTrack={handleTrack}
   188→                />
   189→              );
   190→            })}
   191→          </div>
   192→        </div>
   193→      </div>
   194→
   195→      {/* Keyframes injected via style tag */}
   196→      <style>{`
   197→        @keyframes marquee {
   198→          0% { transform: translateX(0); }
   199→          100% { transform: translateX(-50%); }
   200→        }
   201→      `}</style>
   202→    </div>
   203→  );
   204→}
   205→
   206→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
   207→
   208→function MarqueeChip({
   209→  item,
   210→  tracked,
   211→  tracking,
   212→  onTrack,
   213→}: {
   214→  item: DiscoverItem;
   215→  tracked: boolean;
   216→  tracking: boolean;
   217→  onTrack: (item: DiscoverItem) => void;
   218→}) {
   219→  const ProviderIcon =
   220→    item.provider === "github" ? FaGithub : FaDocker;
   221→
   222→  if (tracked) {
   223→    return (
   224→      <span
   225→        className="inline-flex flex-shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-3 py-1.5"
   226→        style={{
   227→          fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   228→          fontSize: "12px",
   229→          color: "#16a34a",
   230→          backgroundColor: "#f0fdf4",
   231→          border: "1px solid #bbf7d0",
   232→          whiteSpace: "nowrap",
   233→        }}
   234→      >
   235→        <ProviderIcon className="h-3 w-3" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }} />
   236→        {item.full_name}
   237→        <Check className="h-3 w-3" />
   238→      </span>
   239→    );
   240→  }
   241→
   242→  return (
   243→    <button
   244→      onClick={() => onTrack(item)}
   245→      disabled={tracking}
   246→      className="inline-flex flex-shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 transition-all hover:shadow-sm disabled:opacity-70"
   247→      style={{
   248→        fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   249→        fontSize: "12px",
   250→        color: "#111113",
   251→        backgroundColor: "#fafaf9",
   252→        border: "1px solid #e8e8e5",
   253→        whiteSpace: "nowrap",
   254→        cursor: tracking ? "wait" : "pointer",
   255→      }}
   256→    >
   257→      <ProviderIcon className="h-3 w-3" style={{ color: "#6b7280" }} />
   258→      <span>{item.full_name}</span>
   259→      <Star className="h-3 w-3" style={{ color: "#e8601a" }} />
   260→      <span style={{ color: "#6b7280", fontSize: "11px" }}>
   261→        {formatStars(item.stars)}
   262→      </span>
   263→      {tracking ? (
   264→        <Loader2
   265→          className="h-3 w-3 animate-spin"
   266→          style={{ color: "#e8601a" }}
   267→        />
   268→      ) : (
   269→        <Plus className="h-3 w-3" style={{ color: "#e8601a" }} />
   270→      )}
   271→    </button>
   272→  );
   273→}
   274→

     1→"use client";
     2→
     3→import { useState, useCallback } from "react";
     4→import useSWR from "swr";
     5→import { suggestions, onboard } from "@/lib/api/client";
     6→import type { RepoItem, OnboardScan, ScannedDependency, OnboardSelection } from "@/lib/api/types";
     7→
     8→type Step = "pick" | "scanning" | "results" | "applied";
     9→
    10→interface ScanState {
    11→  repoName: string;
    12→  scanId: string;
    13→  status: string;
    14→  results?: ScannedDependency[];
    15→  error?: string;
    16→}
    17→
    18→export function DepsTab() {
    19→  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
    20→  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>("pick");
    21→  const [selectedRepos, setSelectedRepos] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
    22→  const [scans, setScans] = useState<ScanState[]>([]);
    23→  const [appliedCount, setAppliedCount] = useState(0);
    24→
    25→  const { data, isLoading, error } = useSWR(
    26→    loaded ? "suggestions-repos" : null,
    27→    () => suggestions.repos(),
    28→    { revalidateOnFocus: false }
    29→  );
    30→
    31→  const repos = data?.data ?? [];
    32→
    33→  const toggleRepo = useCallback((fullName: string) => {
    34→    setSelectedRepos((prev) => {
    35→      const next = new Set(prev);
    36→      if (next.has(fullName)) next.delete(fullName);
    37→      else next.add(fullName);
    38→      return next;
    39→    });
    40→  }, []);
    41→
    42→  const handleScan = useCallback(async () => {
    43→    const repoNames = Array.from(selectedRepos);
    44→    setStep("scanning");
    45→
    46→    const scanStates: ScanState[] = repoNames.map((name) => ({
    47→      repoName: name,
    48→      scanId: "",
    49→      status: "queued",
    50→    }));
    51→    setScans([...scanStates]);
    52→
    53→    const batchSize = 5;
    54→    for (let i = 0; i < repoNames.length; i += batchSize) {
    55→      const batch = repoNames.slice(i, i + batchSize);
    56→      await Promise.all(
    57→        batch.map(async (repoName, batchIdx) => {
    58→          const idx = i + batchIdx;
    59→          try {
    60→            const repoUrl = `https://github.com/${repoName}`;
    61→            const scanRes = await onboard.scan(repoUrl);
    62→            const scanId = scanRes.data.id;
    63→            scanStates[idx] = { ...scanStates[idx], scanId, status: "processing" };
    64→            setScans([...scanStates]);
    65→
    66→            let scan: OnboardScan;
    67→            do {
    68→              await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2000));
    69→              const pollRes = await onboard.getScan(scanId);
    70→              scan = pollRes.data;
    71→            } while (scan.status === "pending" || scan.status === "processing");
    72→
    73→            if (scan.status === "completed" && scan.results) {
    74→              scanStates[idx] = { ...scanStates[idx], status: "completed", results: scan.results };
    75→            } else {
    76→              scanStates[idx] = { ...scanStates[idx], status: "failed", error: scan.error || "Unknown error" };
    77→            }
    78→          } catch (err: unknown) {
    79→            const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Scan failed";
    80→            scanStates[idx] = { ...scanStates[idx], status: "failed", error: msg };
    81→          }
    82→          setScans([...scanStates]);
    83→        })
    84→      );
    85→    }
    86→
    87→    setStep("results");
    88→  }, [selectedRepos]);
    89→
    90→  const handleApply = useCallback(
    91→    async (selections: { scanId: string; items: OnboardSelection[] }[]) => {
    92→      let total = 0;
    93→      for (const { scanId, items } of selections) {
    94→        if (items.length === 0) continue;
    95→        try {
    96→          const res = await onboard.apply(scanId, items);
    97→          total += res.data.created_sources.length;
    98→        } catch {
    99→          // continue with other scans
   100→        }
   101→      }
   102→      setAppliedCount(total);
   103→      setStep("applied");
   104→    },
   105→    []
   106→  );
   107→
   108→  if (!loaded) {
   109→    return (
   110→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
   111→        <button
   112→          onClick={() => setLoaded(true)}
   113→          className="rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-purple-700 transition-colors"
   114→        >
   115→          Load your repos
   116→        </button>
   117→      </div>
   118→    );
   119→  }
   120→
   121→  if (isLoading) {
   122→    return (
   123→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
   124→        <div className="text-sm text-zinc-400">Loading your repos...</div>
   125→      </div>
   126→    );
   127→  }
   128→
   129→  if (error) {
   130→    return (
   131→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
   132→        <div className="text-sm text-red-400">Failed to load repos. Try again later.</div>
   133→      </div>
   134→    );
   135→  }
   136→
   137→  if (step === "pick") {
   138→    return <RepoPicker repos={repos} selected={selectedRepos} onToggle={toggleRepo} onScan={handleScan} />;
   139→  }
   140→
   141→  if (step === "scanning") {
   142→    return <ScanProgress scans={scans} />;
   143→  }
   144→
   145→  if (step === "results") {
   146→    return <ScanResults scans={scans.filter((s) => s.status === "completed")} onApply={handleApply} />;
   147→  }
   148→
   149→  return (
   150→    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-12 gap-3">
   151→      <div className="text-sm text-green-400">
   152→        Successfully started tracking {appliedCount} dependencies.
   153→      </div>
   154→      <button
   155→        onClick={() => {
   156→          setStep("pick");
   157→          setScans([]);
   158→          setSelectedRepos(new Set());
   159→        }}
   160→        className="text-xs text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200 underline"
   161→      >
   162→        Scan more repos
   163→      </button>
   164→    </div>
   165→  );
   166→}
   167→
   168→function RepoPicker({
   169→  repos,
   170→  selected,
   171→  onToggle,
   172→  onScan,
   173→}: {
   174→  repos: RepoItem[];
   175→  selected: Set<string>;
   176→  onToggle: (name: string) => void;
   177→  onScan: () => void;
   178→}) {
   179→  if (repos.length === 0) {
   180→    return (
   181→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
   182→        <div className="text-sm text-zinc-400">No public repos found.</div>
   183→      </div>
   184→    );
   185→  }
   186→
   187→  return (
   188→    <div>
   189→      <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400 mb-3">Select repos to scan for dependencies:</p>
   190→      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto">
   191→        {repos.map((repo) => (
   192→          <label
   193→            key={repo.full_name}
   194→            className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800 p-3 cursor-pointer hover:border-zinc-600 transition-colors"
   195→          >
   196→            <input
   197→              type="checkbox"
   198→              checked={selected.has(repo.full_name)}
   199→              onChange={() => onToggle(repo.full_name)}
   200→              className="accent-purple-600"
   201→            />
   202→            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
   203→              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-200 truncate">{repo.full_name}</div>
   204→              <div className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
   205→                {repo.pushed_at && `Pushed ${new Date(repo.pushed_at).toLocaleDateString()}`}
   206→                {repo.language && ` · ${repo.language}`}
   207→              </div>
   208→            </div>
   209→          </label>
   210→        ))}
   211→      </div>
   212→      <div className="flex justify-end mt-3">
   213→        <button
   214→          onClick={onScan}
   215→          disabled={selected.size === 0}
   216→          className="rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-purple-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
   217→        >
   218→          Scan Selected ({selected.size})
   219→        </button>
   220→      </div>
   221→    </div>
   222→  );
   223→}
   224→
   225→function ScanProgress({ scans }: { scans: ScanState[] }) {
   226→  return (
   227→    <div className="space-y-2 py-4">
   228→      <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400 mb-3">Scanning repositories for dependencies...</p>
   229→      {scans.map((scan) => (
   230→        <div key={scan.repoName} className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm">
   231→          <span className="w-5 text-center">
   232→            {scan.status === "completed" && "\u2713"}
   233→            {scan.status === "failed" && "\u2717"}
   234→            {(scan.status === "queued" || scan.status === "processing") && "\u23f3"}
   235→          </span>
   236→          <span className={scan.status === "failed" ? "text-red-400" : "text-zinc-300"}>
   237→            {scan.repoName}
   238→          </span>
   239→          {scan.status === "failed" && scan.error && (
   240→            <span className="text-xs text-red-400/70">({scan.error})</span>
   241→          )}
   242→        </div>
   243→      ))}
   244→    </div>
   245→  );
   246→}
   247→
   248→function ScanResults({
   249→  scans,
   250→  onApply,
   251→}: {
   252→  scans: ScanState[];
   253→  onApply: (selections: { scanId: string; items: OnboardSelection[] }[]) => void;
   254→}) {
   255→  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<string>>(() => {
   256→    const all = new Set<string>();
   257→    scans.forEach((scan) =>
   258→      scan.results?.forEach((dep) => all.add(`${scan.scanId}:${dep.name}`))
   259→    );
   260→    return all;
   261→  });
   262→
   263→  const toggle = (key: string) => {
   264→    setSelected((prev) => {
   265→      const next = new Set(prev);
   266→      if (next.has(key)) next.delete(key);
   267→      else next.add(key);
   268→      return next;
   269→    });
   270→  };
   271→
   272→  const handleApply = () => {
   273→    const selections = scans.map((scan) => ({
   274→      scanId: scan.scanId,
   275→      items: (scan.results ?? [])
   276→        .filter((dep) => selected.has(`${scan.scanId}:${dep.name}`))
   277→        .map((dep) => ({
   278→          dep_name: dep.name,
   279→          upstream_repo: dep.upstream_repo,
   280→          provider: dep.provider,
   281→          new_project_name: dep.upstream_repo || dep.name,
   282→        })),
   283→    }));
   284→    onApply(selections);
   285→  };
   286→
   287→  const totalSelected = selected.size;
   288→
   289→  if (scans.length === 0 || scans.every((s) => !s.results?.length)) {
   290→    return (
   291→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-12">
   292→        <div className="text-sm text-zinc-400">No dependencies found in the selected repos.</div>
   293→      </div>
   294→    );
   295→  }
   296→
   297→  return (
   298→    <div>
   299→      {scans.map((scan) => {
   300→        if (!scan.results?.length) return null;
   301→        return (
   302→          <div key={scan.scanId} className="mb-4">
   303→            <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-300 mb-2">
   304→              From {scan.repoName}
   305→            </h4>
   306→            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
   307→              {scan.results.map((dep) => {
   308→                const key = `${scan.scanId}:${dep.name}`;
   309→                return (
   310→                  <label
   311→                    key={key}
   312→                    className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800/50 px-3 py-2 cursor-pointer text-sm"
   313→                  >
   314→                    <input
   315→                      type="checkbox"
   316→                      checked={selected.has(key)}
   317→                      onChange={() => toggle(key)}
   318→                      className="accent-purple-600"
   319→                    />
   320→                    <span className="text-zinc-200">{dep.name}</span>
   321→                    {dep.version && (
   322→                      <span className="text-xs text-zinc-500">{dep.version}</span>
   323→                    )}
   324→                    <span className="ml-auto text-xs text-zinc-500">{dep.ecosystem}</span>
   325→                  </label>
   326→                );
   327→              })}
   328→            </div>
   329→          </div>
   330→        );
   331→      })}
   332→      <div className="flex justify-end mt-3">
   333→        <button
   334→          onClick={handleApply}
   335→          disabled={totalSelected === 0}
   336→          className="rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-purple-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
   337→        >
   338→          Track Selected ({totalSelected})
   339→        </button>
   340→      </div>
   341→    </div>
   342→  );
   343→}
   344→

}

  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState, useCallback } from \"react\";\nimport useSWR, { mutate } from \"swr\";\nimport { Star, Loader2, Check, Plus } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { FaGithub } from \"react-icons/fa\";\nimport { suggestions, projects, sources } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport type { SuggestionItem } from \"@/lib/api/types\";\n\nconst INITIAL_SHOW = 6;\n\nexport function StarsTab() {\n  const [trackingIds, setTrackingIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());\n  const [showAll, setShowAll] = useState(false);\n\n  const { data, isLoading, error } = useSWR(\n    \"suggestions-stars\",\n    () => suggestions.stars(),\n    { revalidateOnFocus: false }\n  );\n\n  const items = data?.data ?? [];\n  const visible = showAll ? items : items.slice(0, INITIAL_SHOW);\n\n  const handleTrack = useCallback(async (item: SuggestionItem) => {\n    const key = item.full_name;\n    setTrackingIds((prev) => new Set(prev).add(key));\n    try {\n      const projRes = await projects.create({\n        name: item.full_name,\n        description: item.description,\n      });\n      const project = projRes.data;\n      const sourceRes = await sources.create(project.id, {\n        provider: \"github\",\n        repository: item.full_name,\n        poll_interval_seconds: 86400,\n        enabled: true,\n      });\n      await sources.poll(sourceRes.data.id);\n      await mutate(\"suggestions-stars\");\n      await mutate(\"projects-list\");\n      await mutate(\"projects-for-dashboard\");\n    } catch (err: unknown) {\n      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : \"Failed to track\";\n      alert(msg);\n    } finally {\n      setTrackingIds((prev) => {\n        const next = new Set(prev);\n        next.delete(key);\n        return next;\n      });\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  if (isLoading) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-10\">\n        <Loader2\n          className=\"h-4 w-4 animate-spin\"\n          style={{ color: \"#e8601a\" }}\n        />\n        <span\n          className=\"ml-2\"\n          style={{\n            fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n            fontSize: \"13px\",\n            color: \"#6b7280\",\n          }}\n        >\n          Loading your starred repos...\n        </span>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (error) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-10\">\n        <span\n          style={{\n            fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n            fontSize: \"13px\",\n            color: \"#ef4444\",\n          }}\n        >\n          Failed to load stars. Try again later.\n        </span>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (items.length === 0) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-10\">\n        <span\n          style={{\n            fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n            fontSize: \"13px\",\n            color: \"#6b7280\",\n          }}\n        >\n          You haven&apos;t starred any public repos on GitHub yet.\n        </span>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div>\n      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3\">\n        {visible.map((item) => {\n          const tracked = item.tracked;\n          const tracking = trackingIds.has(item.full_name);\n\n          return (\n            <div\n              key={item.full_name}\n              className=\"rounded-lg\"\n              style={{\n                border: \"1px solid #e8e8e5\",\n                padding: \"12px\",\n                backgroundColor: tracked ? \"#fafaf9\" : \"#ffffff\",\n                opacity: tracked ? 0.6 : 1,\n              }}\n            >\n              <div className=\"flex items-start justify-between gap-2 mb-2\">\n                <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 min-w-0\">\n                  <FaGithub\n                    className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5 flex-shrink-0\"\n                    style={{ color: \"#6b7280\" }}\n                  />\n                  <span\n                    className=\"truncate\"\n                    style={{\n                      fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                      fontSize: \"13px\",\n                      fontWeight: 600,\n                      color: \"#111113\",\n                    }}\n                  >\n                    {item.full_name}\n                  </span>\n                </div>\n                {tracked ? (\n                  <span\n                    className=\"flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0\"\n                    style={{\n                      fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                      fontSize: \"11px\",\n                      color: \"#16a34a\",\n                    }}\n                  >\n                    <Check className=\"h-3 w-3\" />\n                    Tracked\n                  </span>\n                ) : (\n                  <button\n                    onClick={() => handleTrack(item)}\n                    disabled={tracking}\n                    className=\"shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md px-2.5 py-1 text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-50\"\n                    style={{\n                      backgroundColor: \"#e8601a\",\n                      fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                      fontSize: \"11px\",\n                      fontWeight: 500,\n                      cursor: tracking ? \"wait\" : \"pointer\",\n                    }}\n                  >\n                    {tracking ? (\n                      <Loader2 className=\"h-3 w-3 animate-spin\" />\n                    ) : (\n                      <Plus className=\"h-3 w-3\" />\n                    )}\n                    {tracking ? \"Tracking...\" : \"Track\"}\n                  </button>\n                )}\n              </div>\n              {item.description && (\n                <p\n                  className=\"line-clamp-2 mb-2\"\n                  style={{\n                    fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                    fontSize: \"12px\",\n                    color: \"#6b7280\",\n                    lineHeight: \"1.4\",\n                  }}\n                >\n                  {item.description}\n                </p>\n              )}\n              <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n                <span\n                  className=\"flex items-center gap-1\"\n                  style={{\n                    fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                    fontSize: \"11px\",\n                    color: \"#9ca3af\",\n                  }}\n                >\n                  <Star className=\"h-3 w-3\" style={{ color: \"#e8601a\" }} />\n                  {item.stars.toLocaleString()}\n                </span>\n                {item.language && (\n                  <span\n                    style={{\n                      fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                      fontSize: \"11px\",\n                      color: \"#9ca3af\",\n                    }}\n                  >\n                    {item.language}\n                  </span>\n                )}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          );\n        })}\n      </div>\n      {items.length > INITIAL_SHOW && !showAll && (\n        <div className=\"text-center mt-3\">\n          <button\n            onClick={() => setShowAll(true)}\n            style={{\n              fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n              fontSize: \"12px\",\n              color: \"#e8601a\",\n              background: \"none\",\n              border: \"none\",\n              cursor: \"pointer\",\n            }}\n          >\n            Show all {items.length} starred repos →\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/dashboard/deps-tab.tsx",
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState, useCallback } from \"react\";\nimport useSWR from \"swr\";\nimport { Loader2, Check, AlertCircle } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { suggestions, onboard } from \"@/lib/api/client\";\nimport type { RepoItem, OnboardScan, ScannedDependency, OnboardSelection } from \"@/lib/api/types\";\n\ntype Step = \"pick\" | \"scanning\" | \"results\" | \"applied\";\n\ninterface ScanState {\n  repoName: string;\n  scanId: string;\n  status: string;\n  results?: ScannedDependency[];\n  error?: string;\n}\n\nexport function DepsTab() {\n  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>(\"pick\");\n  const [selectedRepos, setSelectedRepos] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());\n  const [scans, setScans] = useState<ScanState[]>([]);\n  const [appliedCount, setAppliedCount] = useState(0);\n\n  const { data, isLoading, error } = useSWR(\n    \"suggestions-repos\",\n    () => suggestions.repos(),\n    { revalidateOnFocus: false }\n  );\n\n  const repos = data?.data ?? [];\n\n  const toggleRepo = useCallback((fullName: string) => {\n    setSelectedRepos((prev) => {\n      const next = new Set(prev);\n      if (next.has(fullName)) next.delete(fullName);\n      else next.add(fullName);\n      return next;\n    });\n  }, []);\n\n  const handleScan = useCallback(async () => {\n    const repoNames = Array.from(selectedRepos);\n    setStep(\"scanning\");\n\n    const scanStates: ScanState[] = repoNames.map((name) => ({\n      repoName: name,\n      scanId: \"\",\n      status: \"queued\",\n    }));\n    setScans([...scanStates]);\n\n    const batchSize = 5;\n    for (let i = 0; i < repoNames.length; i += batchSize) {\n      const batch = repoNames.slice(i, i + batchSize);\n      await Promise.all(\n        batch.map(async (repoName, batchIdx) => {\n          const idx = i + batchIdx;\n          try {\n            const repoUrl = `https://github.com/${repoName}`;\n            const scanRes = await onboard.scan(repoUrl);\n            const scanId = scanRes.data.id;\n            scanStates[idx] = { ...scanStates[idx], scanId, status: \"processing\" };\n            setScans([...scanStates]);\n\n            let scan: OnboardScan;\n            do {\n              await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2000));\n              const pollRes = await onboard.getScan(scanId);\n              scan = pollRes.data;\n            } while (scan.status === \"pending\" || scan.status === \"processing\");\n\n            if (scan.status === \"completed\" && scan.results) {\n              scanStates[idx] = { ...scanStates[idx], status: \"completed\", results: scan.results };\n            } else {\n              scanStates[idx] = { ...scanStates[idx], status: \"failed\", error: scan.error || \"Unknown error\" };\n            }\n          } catch (err: unknown) {\n            const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : \"Scan failed\";\n            scanStates[idx] = { ...scanStates[idx], status: \"failed\", error: msg };\n          }\n          setScans([...scanStates]);\n        })\n      );\n    }\n\n    setStep(\"results\");\n  }, [selectedRepos]);\n\n  const handleApply = useCallback(\n    async (selections: { scanId: string; items: OnboardSelection[] }[]) => {\n      let total = 0;\n      for (const { scanId, items } of selections) {\n        if (items.length === 0) continue;\n        try {\n          const res = await onboard.apply(scanId, items);\n          total += res.data.created_sources.length;\n        } catch {\n          // continue with other scans\n        }\n      }\n      setAppliedCount(total);\n      setStep(\"applied\");\n    },\n    []\n  );\n\n  if (isLoading) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-10\">\n        <Loader2\n          className=\"h-4 w-4 animate-spin\"\n          style={{ color: \"#e8601a\" }}\n        />\n        <span\n          className=\"ml-2\"\n          style={{\n            fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n            fontSize: \"13px\",\n            color: \"#6b7280\",\n          }}\n        >\n          Loading your repos...\n        </span>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (error) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-10\">\n        <span\n          style={{\n            fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n            fontSize: \"13px\",\n            color: \"#ef4444\",\n          }}\n        >\n          Failed to load repos. Try again later.\n        </span>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (step === \"pick\") {\n    return <RepoPicker repos={repos} selected={selectedRepos} onToggle={toggleRepo} onScan={handleScan} />;\n  }\n\n  if (step === \"scanning\") {\n    return <ScanProgress scans={scans} />;\n  }\n\n  if (step === \"results\") {\n    return <ScanResults scans={scans.filter((s) => s.status === \"completed\")} onApply={handleApply} />;\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-10 gap-3\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n        <Check className=\"h-4 w-4\" style={{ color: \"#16a34a\" }} />\n        <span\n          style={{\n            fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n            fontSize: \"13px\",\n            color: \"#16a34a\",\n          }}\n        >\n          Successfully started tracking {appliedCount} dependencies.\n        </span>\n      </div>\n      <button\n        onClick={() => {\n          setStep(\"pick\");\n          setScans([]);\n          setSelectedRepos(new Set());\n        }}\n        style={{\n          fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n          fontSize: \"12px\",\n          color: \"#e8601a\",\n          background: \"none\",\n          border: \"none\",\n          cursor: \"pointer\",\n        }}\n      >\n        Scan more repos →\n      </button>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction RepoPicker({\n  repos,\n  selected,\n  onToggle,\n  onScan,\n}: {\n  repos: RepoItem[];\n  selected: Set<string>;\n  onToggle: (name: string) => void;\n  onScan: () => void;\n}) {\n  if (repos.length === 0) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-10\">\n        <span\n          style={{\n            fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n            fontSize: \"13px\",\n            color: \"#6b7280\",\n          }}\n        >\n          No public repos found.\n        </span>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div>\n      <p\n        className=\"mb-3\"\n        style={{\n          fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n          fontSize: \"13px\",\n          color: \"#6b7280\",\n        }}\n      >\n        Select repos to scan for dependencies:\n      </p>\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto\">\n        {repos.map((repo) => (\n          <label\n            key={repo.full_name}\n            className=\"flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg p-3 cursor-pointer transition-colors\"\n            style={{\n              border: selected.has(repo.full_name)\n                ? \"1px solid #e8601a\"\n                : \"1px solid #e8e8e5\",\n              backgroundColor: selected.has(repo.full_name)\n                ? \"#fff7ed\"\n                : \"#ffffff\",\n            }}\n          >\n            <input\n              type=\"checkbox\"\n              checked={selected.has(repo.full_name)}\n              onChange={() => onToggle(repo.full_name)}\n              style={{ accentColor: \"#e8601a\" }}\n            />\n            <div className=\"flex-1 min-w-0\">\n              <div\n                className=\"truncate\"\n                style={{\n                  fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                  fontSize: \"13px\",\n                  fontWeight: 600,\n                  color: \"#111113\",\n                }}\n              >\n                {repo.full_name}\n              </div>\n              <div\n                style={{\n                  fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                  fontSize: \"11px\",\n                  color: \"#9ca3af\",\n                }}\n              >\n                {repo.pushed_at && `Pushed ${new Date(repo.pushed_at).toLocaleDateString()}`}\n                {repo.language && ` · ${repo.language}`}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </label>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"flex justify-end mt-3\">\n        <button\n          onClick={onScan}\n          disabled={selected.size === 0}\n          className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-4 py-2 text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed\"\n          style={{\n            backgroundColor: \"#e8601a\",\n            fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n            fontSize: \"13px\",\n            fontWeight: 600,\n          }}\n        >\n          Scan Selected ({selected.size})\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction ScanProgress({ scans }: { scans: ScanState[] }) {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"py-4\">\n      <p\n        className=\"mb-3\"\n        style={{\n          fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n          fontSize: \"13px\",\n          color: \"#6b7280\",\n        }}\n      >\n        Scanning repositories for dependencies...\n      </p>\n      <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n        {scans.map((scan) => (\n          <div\n            key={scan.repoName}\n            className=\"flex items-center gap-3\"\n            style={{\n              fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n              fontSize: \"13px\",\n            }}\n          >\n            <span className=\"w-5 text-center flex-shrink-0\">\n              {scan.status === \"completed\" && (\n                <Check className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5 inline\" style={{ color: \"#16a34a\" }} />\n              )}\n              {scan.status === \"failed\" && (\n                <AlertCircle className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5 inline\" style={{ color: \"#ef4444\" }} />\n              )}\n              {(scan.status === \"queued\" || scan.status === \"processing\") && (\n                <Loader2\n                  className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5 inline animate-spin\"\n                  style={{ color: \"#e8601a\" }}\n                />\n              )}\n            </span>\n            <span style={{ color: scan.status === \"failed\" ? \"#ef4444\" : \"#111113\" }}>\n              {scan.repoName}\n            </span>\n            {scan.status === \"failed\" && scan.error && (\n              <span style={{ fontSize: \"11px\", color: \"#9ca3af\" }}>\n                ({scan.error})\n              </span>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction ScanResults({\n  scans,\n  onApply,\n}: {\n  scans: ScanState[];\n  onApply: (selections: { scanId: string; items: OnboardSelection[] }[]) => void;\n}) {\n  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<string>>(() => {\n    const all = new Set<string>();\n    scans.forEach((scan) =>\n      scan.results?.forEach((dep) => all.add(`${scan.scanId}:${dep.name}`))\n    );\n    return all;\n  });\n\n  const toggle = (key: string) => {\n    setSelected((prev) => {\n      const next = new Set(prev);\n      if (next.has(key)) next.delete(key);\n      else next.add(key);\n      return next;\n    });\n  };\n\n  const handleApply = () => {\n    const selections = scans.map((scan) => ({\n      scanId: scan.scanId,\n      items: (scan.results ?? [])\n        .filter((dep) => selected.has(`${scan.scanId}:${dep.name}`))\n        .map((dep) => ({\n          dep_name: dep.name,\n          upstream_repo: dep.upstream_repo,\n          provider: dep.provider,\n          new_project_name: dep.upstream_repo || dep.name,\n        })),\n    }));\n    onApply(selections);\n  };\n\n  const totalSelected = selected.size;\n\n  if (scans.length === 0 || scans.every((s) => !s.results?.length)) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-10\">\n        <span\n          style={{\n            fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n            fontSize: \"13px\",\n            color: \"#6b7280\",\n          }}\n        >\n          No dependencies found in the selected repos.\n        </span>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div>\n      {scans.map((scan) => {\n        if (!scan.results?.length) return null;\n        return (\n          <div key={scan.scanId} className=\"mb-4\">\n            <h4\n              className=\"mb-2\"\n              style={{\n                fontFamily: \"var(--font-fraunces)\",\n                fontSize: \"13px\",\n                fontWeight: 600,\n                color: \"#111113\",\n              }}\n            >\n              From {scan.repoName}\n            </h4>\n            <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1\">\n              {scan.results.map((dep) => {\n                const key = `${scan.scanId}:${dep.name}`;\n                return (\n                  <label\n                    key={key}\n                    className=\"flex items-center gap-3 rounded-md px-3 py-2 cursor-pointer\"\n                    style={{\n                      border: \"1px solid #e8e8e5\",\n                      backgroundColor: selected.has(key) ? \"#fff7ed\" : \"#fafaf9\",\n                      fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                      fontSize: \"13px\",\n                    }}\n                  >\n                    <input\n                      type=\"checkbox\"\n                      checked={selected.has(key)}\n                      onChange={() => toggle(key)}\n                      style={{ accentColor: \"#e8601a\" }}\n                    />\n                    <span style={{ color: \"#111113\" }}>{dep.name}</span>\n                    {dep.version && (\n                      <span style={{ fontSize: \"11px\", color: \"#9ca3af\" }}>\n                        {dep.version}\n                      </span>\n                    )}\n                    <span\n                      className=\"ml-auto\"\n                      style={{ fontSize: \"11px\", color: \"#9ca3af\" }}\n                    >\n                      {dep.ecosystem}\n                    </span>\n                  </label>\n                );\n              })}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        );\n      })}\n      <div className=\"flex justify-end mt-3\">\n        <button\n          onClick={handleApply}\n          disabled={totalSelected === 0}\n          className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-4 py-2 text-white transition-opacity hover:opacity-90 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed\"\n          style={{\n            backgroundColor: \"#e8601a\",\n            fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n            fontSize: \"13px\",\n            fontWeight: 600,\n          }}\n        >\n          Track Selected ({totalSelected})\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

Perfect! Now I have a complete understanding. Let me create a comprehensive summary document:

## Complete Onboard Flow Summary

### Overview
The onboard page is a four-step wizard that lets users scan GitHub repositories, detect dependencies, and configure them for release tracking. It's a fully client-side React component with real-time polling and state management.

---

### 1. **Page Location & Structure**
**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/onboard/page.tsx`

Architecture:
- Wrapped in `Suspense` boundary for SSR compatibility
- Main logic in `OnboardPageInner()` component
- Uses local React state for step progression
- Integrates with Next.js router/searchParams for navigation

---

### 2. **Visual UI Pattern & State Flow**

#### **Four-Step State Machine:**
```
"input" → "scanning" → "results" → "applied"
```

#### **Step 1: Input (GitHub Repo URL)**
- **UI:** Minimal card with text input + Scan button
- **Styling:**
  - Header: Fraunces font, 24px bold, dark gray
  - Subheader: DM Sans, 13px, lighter gray
  - Input: Bordered box, JetBrains Mono font
  - Button: Orange (#e8601a) with Search icon
  - Helper text explains file types scanned (go.mod, package.json, etc.)
- **Interaction:** Enter URL or paste, press Enter or click Scan button
- **Storage:** `repoUrl` state
- **Auto-start:** Supports `?repo=owner/repo` query param for programmatic scanning

#### **Step 2: Scanning (Real-time Progress)**
- **UI:** Centered spinner with progress indicators
- **Styling:**
  - Orange spinner (#e8601a) in pale orange container
  - Sub-heading: "Analyzing dependency files..."
- **Progress Display:** Three-step flow with connectors:
  1. "Queued" → 2. "Fetching files" → 3. "Extracting deps"
  - Completed steps show green check mark
  - Active step shows spinning orange loader
  - Future steps show gray dot
- **Connectors:** Gray lines that turn green on completion
- **Elapsed time:** Displays seconds elapsed + current repo URL
- **Polling:** 2-second intervals checking `/onboard/scans/{id}` for status
- **Auto-transition:** Moves to "results" when scan status = "completed"

#### **Step 3: Results (Dependency Selection Table)**
- **Layout:** Summary header + full-width table
- **Summary Bar:**
  - Shows "Found X dependencies. Selected: Y"
  - "Track Selected (Y)" button (orange, right-aligned)
  - Button disabled if no items selected OR already applying
- **Table Structure:**
  - **Columns:**
    1. Checkbox (select all in header)
    2. **Dependency** — Name in monospace (JetBrains Mono)
    3. **Version** — Version string in gray badge
    4. **Ecosystem** — Colorized pill badge (see below)
    5. **Source** — Upstream repo URL in monospace
    6. **Project** — Dropdown to assign or create project
  
- **Ecosystem Colors** (tailored per-language):
  - **Go:** Cyan (#ecfeff bg, #0e7490 text)
  - **npm:** Red (#fef2f2 bg, #b91c1c text)
  - **PyPI:** Yellow (#fefce8 bg, #a16207 text)
  - **Cargo:** Orange (#fff7ed bg, #c2410c text)
  - **RubyGems:** Pink (#fdf2f8 bg, #be185d text)
  - **Maven/Gradle/Docker:** Blue (#eff6ff bg, #1d4ed8 text)
  - **Other:** Gray (#f3f3f1 bg, #6b7280 text)

- **Row Selection:**
  - Unchecked rows fade to 50% opacity
  - Header checkbox toggles all
  - Click per-row to select individually
  - Accent color is orange (#e8601a)

- **Project Assignment (Key Feature):**
  - Dropdown per dependency
  - Options:
    - **"Create new project"** (default) — Creates with sanitized name (replaces `/` with `-`)
    - **Existing projects** — Fetched on page load via `projects.list(1, 200)`
  - State stored in `projectAssignments[i]` as `{ mode: "new" | "existing", projectId?: string, newName?: string }`

- **Empty Results State:**
  - Icon: `PackageSearch` (lucide-react)
  - Message: "No dependencies detected in this repository."
  - Button: "Try another repository" (links back to input step)

#### **Step 4: Applied (Success Summary)**
- **Green Success Alert:**
  - Check icon + "Successfully created X sources and Y projects"
  - Green background (#f0fdf4), green border, green text (#15803d)

- **Warning Box (if skipped items):**
  - Yellow background (#fefce8)
  - Shows list of skipped items with reason
  - Heading: "Skipped (N):"

- **Action Buttons (Bottom):**
  1. **"View Projects"** (orange) → Routes to `/projects`
  2. **"Scan Another Repo"** (bordered gray) → Resets to input step

---

### 3. **Component Hierarchy**

```
OnboardPage (SSR wrapper)
└── OnboardPageInner (main logic)
    ├── Error Alert (conditional)
    ├── Step 1: Input Section
    │   └── Input + Scan Button
    └── Step 2: Scanning Section
    │   ├── Spinner
    │   └── ScanStep × 3 (custom components)
    │       └── StepConnector × 2 (custom components)
    │
    └── Step 3a: Results Empty State
    │   ├── PackageSearch Icon
    │   └── "Try Another Repo" Button
    │
    └── Step 3b: Results with Dependencies
    │   ├── Summary Bar
    │   ├── Select All Checkbox
    │   └── Table
    │       └── Rows (mapped from deps)
    │           ├── Checkbox
    │           ├── Dep Name (monospace)
    │           ├── Version Badge
    │           ├── Ecosystem Pill
    │           ├── Source URL
    │           └── Project Dropdown
    │
    └── Step 4: Applied Success
        ├── Success Alert
        ├── Skipped Items List (conditional)
        └── Action Buttons

Custom Components (defined in page.tsx):
- ScanStep({ label, done, active })
- StepConnector({ done })
```

---

### 4. **Data Flow & API Integration**

**API Endpoints Used:**
- `POST /onboard/scan` — Start scan with repo URL
- `GET /onboard/scans/{id}` — Poll scan status (every 2 seconds)
- `GET /projects?page=1&per_page=200` — Fetch existing projects (on load)
- `POST /onboard/scans/{id}/apply` — Apply selections and create projects/sources

**Types:**
```typescript
OnboardScan {
  id: string;
  repo_url: string;
  status: "pending" | "processing" | "completed" | "failed";
  results?: ScannedDependency[];
  error?: string;
}

ScannedDependency {
  name: string;
  version: string;
  ecosystem: string;
  upstream_repo: string;
  provider: string;
}

OnboardSelection {
  dep_name: string;
  upstream_repo: string;
  provider: string;
  project_id?: string;           // Either this...
  new_project_name?: string;     // ...or this
}

OnboardApplyResult {
  created_projects: Project[];
  created_sources: Source[];
  skipped: string[];
}
```

---

### 5. **Key UI Behaviors**

**Selection Logic:**
- `selections` object tracks which deps are checked (`Record<number, boolean>`)
- `projectAssignments` tracks each dep's project target
- When scan completes, ALL items are auto-checked with default "create new" assignments
- Sanitization: `/` in names replaced with `-` (e.g., `@org/pkg` → `@org-pkg`)

**Polling & Transitions:**
- 2-second polling interval during scanning
- Auto-stops polling on completion or failure
- Elapsed time counter updates every 1 second
- Failed scans show error alert and revert to input step

**Disable States:**
- Scan button disabled if URL is empty
- Track button disabled if no items selected OR already applying
- Ellipsis loader shows during apply

**Error Handling:**
- Top-level error alert box (red banner) for user-facing messages
- Try-catch with fallback "Failed to..." message
- Scans can return 409 status (existing scan) — treated as success and returned

---

### 6. **Design System Notes**

**Fonts:**
- **Headings:** Fraunces (serif, variable)
- **Body:** DM Sans (sans-serif)
- **Code/URLs:** JetBrains Mono (monospace)

**Color Palette:**
- **Primary Action:** Orange (#e8601a)
- **Success:** Green (#16a34a, #f0fdf4 bg)
- **Warning:** Yellow (#a16207, #fefce8 bg)
- **Error:** Red (#b91c1c, #fef2f2 bg)
- **Neutral:** Grays (#111113, #6b7280, #9ca3af, #e8e8e5)
- **Ecosystem-specific:** See Step 3 colors above

**Spacing & Sizing:**
- Gaps: 2, 3, 4, 6 units (Tailwind)
- Border-radius: Mostly rounded-md (6px)
- Icons: Lucide-react (h-4 to h-8, w-4 to w-8)

---

### 7. **No Direct Child Components Used**

This page is **self-contained** — it doesn't import or use any separate component files from `/components`. Everything is:
- Page-level state management
- Inline styling with object props
- Built-in HTML elements (input, button, select, checkbox, table)
- Lucide-react icons (Search, Loader2, Check, PackageSearch, ArrowRight)
- Tailwind CSS classes

---

### Summary
The onboard flow is a **focused, wizard-style experience** designed for rapid onboarding. It uses a **table-based UI for dependency selection**, with color-coded badges for ecosystems and **in-line project assignment dropdowns**. The real-time progress indicators during scanning and the success summary at the end create a clear visual narrative of the entire process.

     9→
    10→type Step = "input" | "scanning" | "results" | "applied";
    11→
    12→const ecosystemColors: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string; border: string }> = {
    13→  go: { bg: "#ecfeff", text: "#0e7490", border: "#a5f3fc" },
    14→  npm: { bg: "#fef2f2", text: "#b91c1c", border: "#fecaca" },

    18→  maven: { bg: "#eff6ff", text: "#1d4ed8", border: "#bfdbfe" },
    19→  gradle: { bg: "#eff6ff", text: "#1d4ed8", border: "#bfdbfe" },
    20→  docker: { bg: "#eff6ff", text: "#1d4ed8", border: "#bfdbfe" },
    21→  other: { bg: "#f3f3f1", text: "#6b7280", border: "#e8e8e5" },
    22→};
    23→
    24→export default function OnboardPage() {
    25→  return (
    26→    <Suspense>
    27→      <OnboardPageInner />
    28→    </Suspense>
    29→  );
    30→}
    31→
    32→function OnboardPageInner() {
    33→  const router = useRouter();
    34→  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
    35→  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>("input");
    36→  const [repoUrl, setRepoUrl] = useState("");
    37→  const [scanId, setScanId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    38→  const [scan, setScan] = useState<OnboardScan | null>(null);
    39→  const [selections, setSelections] = useState<Record<number, boolean>>({});
    40→  const [projectAssignments, setProjectAssignments] = useState<Record<number, { mode: "new" | "existing"; projectId?: string; newName?: string }>>({});
    41→  const [applying, setApplying] = useState(false);
    42→  const [applyResult, setApplyResult] = useState<{ created_projects: Project[]; created_sources: Source[]; skipped: string[] } | null>(null);
    43→  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    44→  const [autoStarted, setAutoStarted] = useState(false);
    45→  const [elapsed, setElapsed] = useState(0);
    46→
    47→  // Fetch existing projects for the dropdown
    48→  const { data: projectsResp } = useSWR("projects-all", () => projectsApi.list(1, 200));
    49→  const existingProjects = projectsResp?.data ?? [];
    50→
    51→  // Poll scan status
    52→  useEffect(() => {
    53→    if (step !== "scanning" || !scanId) return;
    54→    const interval = setInterval(async () => {
    55→      try {
    56→        const resp = await onboardApi.getScan(scanId);
    57→        setScan(resp.data);
    58→        if (resp.data.status === "completed") {
    59→          setStep("results");
    60→          const deps = resp.data.results ?? [];
    61→          const sel: Record<number, boolean> = {};
    62→          const assign: Record<number, { mode: "new" | "existing"; newName?: string }> = {};
    63→          deps.forEach((d, i) => {
    64→            sel[i] = true;
    65→            assign[i] = { mode: "new", newName: d.name.replace(/\//g, "-") };
    66→          });
    67→          setSelections(sel);
    68→          setProjectAssignments(assign);
    69→          clearInterval(interval);
    70→        } else if (resp.data.status === "failed") {
    71→          setError(resp.data.error || "Scan failed");
    72→          setStep("input");
    73→          clearInterval(interval);
    74→        }
    75→      } catch {
    76→        // Keep polling
    77→      }
    78→    }, 2000);
    79→    return () => clearInterval(interval);
    80→  }, [step, scanId]);
    81→
    82→  // Elapsed time counter during scanning
    83→  useEffect(() => {
    84→    if (step !== "scanning") { setElapsed(0); return; }
    85→    const start = Date.now();
    86→    const timer = setInterval(() => setElapsed(Math.floor((Date.now() - start) / 1000)), 1000);
    87→    return () => clearInterval(timer);
    88→  }, [step]);
    89→
    90→  // Auto-start scan from ?repo= query param
    91→  useEffect(() => {
    92→    const repo = searchParams.get("repo");
    93→    if (repo && !autoStarted) {
    94→      setAutoStarted(true);
    95→      setRepoUrl(repo);
    96→      beginScan(repo);
    97→    }
    98→  }, [searchParams, autoStarted]);
    99→
   100→  const beginScan = async (url: string) => {
   101→    setError(null);
   102→    try {
   103→      const resp = await onboardApi.scan(url);
   104→      const scanData = resp.data;
   105→      setScanId(scanData.id);
   106→      setScan(scanData);
   107→      // If scan is already completed (resuming a finished scan), go straight to results
   108→      if (scanData.status === "completed") {
   109→        const deps = scanData.results ?? [];
   110→        const sel: Record<number, boolean> = {};
   111→        const assign: Record<number, { mode: "new" | "existing"; newName?: string }> = {};
   112→        deps.forEach((d, i) => {
   113→          sel[i] = true;
   114→          assign[i] = { mode: "new", newName: d.name.replace(/\//g, "-") };
   115→        });
   116→        setSelections(sel);
   117→        setProjectAssignments(assign);
   118→        setStep("results");
   119→      } else if (scanData.status === "failed") {
   120→        setError(scanData.error || "Scan failed");
   121→        setStep("input");
   122→      } else {
   123→        setStep("scanning");
   124→      }
   125→    } catch (e: unknown) {
   126→      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Failed to start scan");
   127→    }
   128→  };
   129→
   130→  const startScan = () => {
   131→    if (repoUrl.trim()) beginScan(repoUrl);
   132→  };
   133→
   134→  const applySelections = async () => {
   135→    if (!scan?.results || !scanId) return;
   136→    setApplying(true);
   137→    setError(null);
   138→
   139→    const sels: OnboardSelection[] = [];
   140→    scan.results.forEach((dep, i) => {

   142→      const assign = projectAssignments[i];
   143→      sels.push({

   147→        project_id: assign?.mode === "existing" ? assign.projectId : undefined,
   148→        new_project_name: assign?.mode === "new" ? (assign.newName || dep.name.replace(/\//g, "-")) : undefined,
   149→      });
   150→    });
   151→
   152→    try {
   153→      const resp = await onboardApi.apply(scanId, sels);
   154→      setApplyResult(resp.data);
   155→      setStep("applied");
   156→    } catch (e: unknown) {
   157→      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Failed to apply selections");
   158→    } finally {
   159→      setApplying(false);
   160→    }
   161→  };
   162→
   163→  const deps = scan?.results ?? [];
   164→  const selectedCount = Object.values(selections).filter(Boolean).length;
   165→
   166→  return (

   201→
   202→      {/* Step 1: Input */}

   229→                onBlur={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = "#e8e8e5"; }}

   244→            </p>

   476→
   477→      {/* Step 4: Applied */}
   478→      {step === "applied" && applyResult && (

   509→              </ul>
   510→            </div>
   511→          )}
   512→
   513→          <div className="flex gap-3">
   514→            <button
   515→              onClick={() => router.push("/projects")}
