   540→/* --- Progress step components --- */
   541→
   542→function ScanStep({ label, done, active }: { label: string; done: boolean; active: boolean }) {
   543→  return (
   544→    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
   545→      <div
   546→        className="flex items-center justify-center rounded-full"
   547→        style={{
   548→          width: 18,
   549→          height: 18,
   550→          backgroundColor: done ? "#f0fdf4" : active ? "#fff7ed" : "#f3f3f1",
   551→          border: `1.5px solid ${done ? "#16a34a" : active ? "#e8601a" : "#e8e8e5"}`,
   552→        }}
   553→      >
   554→        {done ? (
   555→          <Check className="h-3 w-3" style={{ color: "#16a34a" }} />
   556→        ) : active ? (
   557→          <Loader2 className="h-3 w-3 animate-spin" style={{ color: "#e8601a" }} />
   558→        ) : (
   559→          <div className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: "#d1d5db" }} />
   560→        )}
   561→      </div>
   562→      <span
   563→        className="text-[12px]"
   564→        style={{
   565→          color: done ? "#16a34a" : active ? "#111113" : "#9ca3af",
   566→          fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
   567→          fontWeight: active ? 500 : 400,
   568→        }}
   569→      >
   570→        {label}
   571→      </span>
   572→    </div>
   573→  );
   574→}
   575→
   576→function StepConnector({ done }: { done: boolean }) {
   577→  return (
   578→    <div
   579→      className="h-px"
   580→      style={{
   581→        width: 24,
   582→        backgroundColor: done ? "#16a34a" : "#e8e8e5",
   583→      }}
   584→    />
   585→  );
   586→}

Now I understand exactly what the user wants. The deps tab should:

1. **No multi-select repos** — pick one repo at a time (like Quick Onboard picks one URL)
2. **Reuse the same onboard-style table UI** for scan results (with ecosystem color pills, version badges, project dropdowns, etc.)

Let me rewrite `deps-tab.tsx` to follow the onboard pattern: user clicks a repo → scans it → shows results in the same table UI → can apply, then scan another.

> AGENT

So right now the dashboard has:
1. **SuggestionsSection** (Your Stars | Your Dependencies tabs) — only for GitHub-authenticated users
2. **DiscoverySection** (trending marquee) — for unauthenticated/dev users
3. **Quick Onboard** (inline URL input → navigates to `/onboard` page)

Here's my thinking on the unified arrangement:

**One card, three tabs** — all inside a single "Quick Onboard" container:

| Scan URL | Your Stars | Your Dependencies |
|----------|-----------|-------------------|

- **Scan URL** (default tab) — the repo URL input + scan button, same as current Quick Onboard but inline instead of navigating away
- **Your Stars** — current stars tab (auto-loads, track buttons)
- **Your Dependencies** — current deps tab (pick repo, scan, table results)

For unauthenticated users, the stars/deps tabs simply don't appear — they just see the single "Scan URL" input (like today's Quick Onboard).

This way:
- All "add repos to track" actions live in one place
- No separate sections competing for attention
- The Trending marquee can stay above it (for discovery/browsing) or be removed
- Authenticated users get 3 tabs, unauthenticated get just the URL input

**Alternative**: Put Trending *inside* the tabs as a 4th tab for unauthenticated users instead of a separate section — but that might over-complicate it.

What do you think — does the 3-tab approach work? And should the Trending marquee stay as a separate section above, or fold into this too?

```
Perfect! I now have a complete picture of the dashboard layout and component structure. Let me provide you with a comprehensive summary.

---

## Dashboard Layout & Component Hierarchy

### 1. **Dashboard Page Structure** (`web/app/page.tsx`)

**Conditional Rendering Logic:**
- **Lines 74-78**: The page shows either `SuggestionsSection` OR `DiscoverySection` based on user authentication:
  - **SuggestionsSection**: Shown when `user.github_login` exists AND is not "dev"
  - **DiscoverySection**: Shown for unauthenticated or "dev" users

**Component Order (top to bottom):**
1. "Dashboard" title
2. Either SuggestionsSection or DiscoverySection
3. **Quick Onboard inline input** (lines 80-136) — always visible
4. Stats Cards, Release Trend Chart, Unified Feed (only if projects exist)
5. Empty state or loading indicator

---

### 2. **Quick Onboard Inline Input** (Lines 80-136 in `page.tsx`)

**Structure:**
- Label: "Quick Onboard"
- Single text input field
- Search button
- Helper text: "Scan a repo for dependencies"

**Current Navigation Flow:**
- When user presses Enter or clicks "Scan" button:
  - Takes the `repoUrl` from input
  - Navigates to `/onboard?repo={encodeURIComponent(repoUrl.trim())}`
  - The onboard page then auto-starts the scan via the `?repo=` query parameter

**Props/State:**
- Local state: `repoUrl` (controlled input)
- Router: `useRouter()` for navigation

---

### 3. **SuggestionsSection** (`web/components/dashboard/suggestions-section.tsx`)

**Props:** None

**State Management:**
- Local state: `tab` — tracks which tab is active ("stars" or "deps")

**Component Hierarchy:**
```
SuggestionsSection (tabbed container)
├── Tabs (ui-only, no state)
│   ├── "Your Stars" button (onClick → setTab("stars"))
│   └── "Your Dependencies" button (onClick → setTab("deps"))
└── Tab Content
    ├── StarsTab (when tab === "stars")
    └── DepsTab (when tab === "deps")
```

**Styling:**
- White card with border
- Underline animation on active tab (orange #e8601a)

---

### 4. **StarsTab** (`web/components/dashboard/stars-tab.tsx`)

**Props:** None

**State Management:**
- `trackingIds`: Set<string> — tracks which repos are currently being tracked
- `showAll`: boolean — controls initial 6 vs all items display
- **SWR**: `"suggestions-stars"` key that fetches user's starred repos

**Data Flow:**
1. Fetches data via `suggestions.stars()` API
2. Displays items in a 3-column grid
3. For each item:
   - If already tracked: displays "✓ Tracked" badge
   - If not tracked: displays "Track" button with Plus icon
   - Shows description, star count, and language

**Action (`handleTrack`):**
- Takes a `SuggestionItem`
- Creates a new project → creates a GitHub source → polls it
- Mutates: `"suggestions-stars"`, `"projects-list"`, `"projects-for-dashboard"`

**Pagination:**
- Shows first 6 items by default
- "Show all X starred repos →" link expands to full list

---

### 5. **DepsTab** (`web/components/dashboard/deps-tab.tsx`)

**Props:** None

**State Management:**
- `step`: "pick" | "scanning" | "results" | "applied"
- `scanId`: string | null
- `scan`: OnboardScan | null (current scan object)
- `selections`: Record<number, boolean> (selected deps for tracking)
- `projectAssignments`: Record<number, { mode, projectId?, newName? }>
- `applying`: boolean (applying selections in progress)
- `applyResult`: null | { created_projects, created_sources, skipped }
- `error`: string | null
- `scanningRepo`: string
- `elapsed`: number (seconds elapsed during scan)
- **SWR**: 
  - `"suggestions-repos"` — fetches user's public repos
  - `"projects-all"` — fetches existing projects for dropdown

**Step Flow:**
1. **"pick"** (default): User selects from list of their public repos to scan
2. **"scanning"**: Polling scan status every 2 seconds
3. **"results"**: Shows dependency table with checkboxes and project assignment dropdowns
4. **"applied"**: Shows success message and option to scan another repo

**Action (`handleScanRepo`):**
- Takes a repo from the list
- Constructs full GitHub URL
- Calls `onboard.scan(repoUrl)`
- Auto-selects all deps and sets them to "new project" mode by default

**Apply Action (`applySelections`):**
- Builds `OnboardSelection[]` array from selected items
- Calls `onboard.apply(scanId, selections)`
- Transitions to "applied" step on success

---

### 6. **DiscoverySection** (`web/components/dashboard/discovery-section.tsx`)

**Props:** None

**State Management:**
- `trackingIds`: Set<string> — tracks in-progress tracking operations
- `paused`: boolean — pauses/resumes marquee animation on hover
- **SWR**:
  - `"discover-github"` — fetches trending GitHub repos
  - `"projects-list"` — fetches user's existing projects

**Component Structure:**
```
DiscoverySection
├── Label: "Trending"
├── Marquee Container (infinite scrolling)
│   └── MarqueeChip (duplicated items for seamless loop)
│       ├── If tracked: green badge with checkmark
│       └── If not tracked: clickable chip with track button
└── CSS Keyframes: marquee animation
```

**Visual Behavior:**
- Marquee animation speed scales with item count (min 60s, base 6s per item)
- Pauses on hover
- Fade edges on left/right
- Green "tracked" state, orange "track" action button

**Action (`handleTrack`):**
- Similar to StarsTab
- Creates project → creates source (GitHub or Docker) → polls
- Mutates: `"projects-list"`, `"projects-for-dashboard"`

**Visibility:**
- Returns `null` if no items to display
- Shows skeleton loaders during initial fetch
- Only visible to unauthenticated or "dev" users

---

### 7. **Onboard Page** (`web/app/onboard/page.tsx`)

**Structure:**
- Wrapper with Suspense (required for `useSearchParams()`)
- Inner component: `OnboardPageInner`

**Auto-Start Logic:**
- Checks for `?repo=` query param on mount
- If present: auto-starts scan via `beginScan(repo)`
- Sets `autoStarted` flag to prevent re-triggering

**State Management:**
- `step`: "input" | "scanning" | "results" | "applied"
- `repoUrl`: string (user input or from query param)
- `scanId`: string | null
- `scan`: OnboardScan | null
- `selections`: Record<number, boolean>
- `projectAssignments`: Record<number, {...}>
- `applying`, `applyResult`, `error`, `elapsed`: similar to DepsTab
- **SWR**: `"projects-all"` for project dropdown

**Step Flow:**
1. **"input"**: Text input for GitHub URL (manual entry)
2. **"scanning"**: Progress indicator with status steps
3. **"results"**: 
   - Empty state if no deps found
   - Full table if deps found (identical to DepsTab results)
4. **"applied"**: Success summary with links to projects or option to scan another repo

**Key Difference from DepsTab:**
- Accepts manual URL input instead of picking from user's repos
- All dependencies selected by default
- Projects defaulted to "create new"

---

## Summary of Props & State Management

| Component | Props | Key SWR Keys | Local State |
|-----------|-------|--------------|-------------|
| **SuggestionsSection** | None | — | `tab` |
| **StarsTab** | None | `"suggestions-stars"` | `trackingIds`, `showAll` |
| **DepsTab** | None | `"suggestions-repos"`, `"projects-all"` | `step`, `scanId`, `scan`, `selections`, `projectAssignments`, `applying`, `applyResult`, `error`, `scanningRepo`, `elapsed` |
| **DiscoverySection** | None | `"discover-github"`, `"projects-list"` | `trackingIds`, `paused` |
| **OnboardPage** | None | `"projects-all"` | `step`, `repoUrl`, `scanId`, `scan`, `selections`, `projectAssignments`, `applying`, `applyResult`, `error`, `autoStarted`, `elapsed` |

## Current Quick Onboard Flow

```
Dashboard page.tsx
    ↓
User types repo URL into Quick Onboard input
    ↓
User presses Enter or clicks "Scan"
    ↓
router.push(`/onboard?repo=${encodeURIComponent(repoUrl)}`)
    ↓
OnboardPageInner component mounts
    ↓
useEffect detects ?repo= param
    ↓
Calls beginScan(repo) → auto-transitions to "scanning" step
    ↓
Polls scan status every 2s
    ↓
On completion → "results" step with dependency table
```

## Implementation Plan: Unified Dashboard Onboard Card

### 1. Analysis of Current Duplication

Three places currently duplicate the scan-results-apply flow and the `ecosystemColors` map:

| Code | Location | ecosystemColors | Scan flow | Results table | Apply flow |
|------|----------|----------------|-----------|---------------|------------|
| DepsTab | `/web/components/dashboard/deps-tab.tsx` | Lines 11-21 (inline) | Lines 85-114 | Lines 301-449 | Lines 116-143 |
| Onboard Page | `/web/app/onboard/page.tsx` | Lines 12-22 (duplicate) | Lines 100-128 | Lines 324-474 | Lines 134-161 |

The results tables are essentially identical in structure (same columns: checkbox, dependency, version, ecosystem, source, project). The scan/poll/apply logic is also nearly identical. The key differences:

- **DepsTab** starts from a repo picker (list of user's GitHub repos), then scans by constructing `https://github.com/${repo.full_name}`.
- **Onboard page** starts from a free-text URL input, uses `useSearchParams` for `?repo=` auto-start, and has `ScanStep`/`StepConnector` progress indicators.
- **DepsTab** has a "Pick another repo" back-link; **Onboard page** has "View Projects" / "Scan Another Repo" after apply.
- Neither DepsTab nor Onboard page calls `mutate("projects-for-dashboard")` after apply (both should, for consistency).

### 2. Design Decisions

**Q1: Should we create a new "ScanUrlTab" component, or inline it?**

**Answer: Create a new `ScanUrlTab` component** at `web/components/dashboard/scan-url-tab.tsx`. Reasons:
- Keeps each tab as a self-contained, testable unit (consistent with StarsTab and DepsTab pattern).
- The scan-url flow has its own state machine (input -> scanning -> results -> applied) that would bloat `suggestions-section.tsx` if inlined.
- Single responsibility: the parent card only handles tab switching.

**Q2: Should we extract the shared ecosystemColors + results table?**

**Answer: Yes, extract two shared pieces:**
1. `ecosystemColors` constant into `web/components/dashboard/shared/ecosystem-colors.ts`.
2. A `ScanResultsTable` component into `web/components/dashboard/shared/scan-results-table.tsx` that encapsulates the checkbox table, selection state management, and project assignment dropdowns. Both DepsTab, ScanUrlTab, and the onboard page will import it.

This avoids triple-maintaining the identical 130-line table. The `ScanResultsTable` takes props:
- `deps: ScannedDependency[]`
- `selections: Record<number, boolean>` + `onSelectionsChange`
- `projectAssignments: Record<number, ...>` + `onProjectAssignmentsChange`
- `existingProjects: Project[]`

The "Track Selected" button and apply logic remain in the parent (DepsTab/ScanUrlTab/OnboardPage) because the post-apply behavior differs per context.

**Q3: How should page.tsx change?**

**Answer:**
- For **unauthenticated users** (`!user?.github_login || github_login === "dev"`): show `DiscoverySection` (unchanged) then the unified card with only the "Scan URL" tab visible.
- For **authenticated users**: show only the unified card with all 3 tabs (Scan URL, Your Stars, Your Dependencies). No DiscoverySection for them (matches current behavior where authenticated users see SuggestionsSection instead of DiscoverySection).
- Remove the separate "Quick Onboard inline" `<div>` block entirely from `page.tsx` (lines 80-136).

### 3. Step-by-Step File Changes

#### Step 1: Create `web/components/dashboard/shared/ecosystem-colors.ts`

New file. Extract the `ecosystemColors` constant:

```typescript
// web/components/dashboard/shared/ecosystem-colors.ts
export const ecosystemColors: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string; border: string }> = {
  go:       { bg: "#ecfeff", text: "#0e7490", border: "#a5f3fc" },
  npm:      { bg: "#fef2f2", text: "#b91c1c", border: "#fecaca" },
  pypi:     { bg: "#fefce8", text: "#a16207", border: "#fde68a" },
  cargo:    { bg: "#fff7ed", text: "#c2410c", border: "#fed7aa" },
  rubygems: { bg: "#fdf2f8", text: "#be185d", border: "#fbcfe8" },
  maven:    { bg: "#eff6ff", text: "#1d4ed8", border: "#bfdbfe" },
  gradle:   { bg: "#eff6ff", text: "#1d4ed8", border: "#bfdbfe" },
  docker:   { bg: "#eff6ff", text: "#1d4ed8", border: "#bfdbfe" },
  other:    { bg: "#f3f3f1", text: "#6b7280", border: "#e8e8e5" },
};
```

#### Step 2: Create `web/components/dashboard/shared/scan-results-table.tsx`

New file. Extract the results table (currently ~120 lines duplicated in DepsTab lines 320-437 and onboard page lines 342-473).

Props interface:

```typescript
import type { ScannedDependency, Project } from "@/lib/api/types";

interface ScanResultsTableProps {
  deps: ScannedDependency[];
  selections: Record<number, boolean>;
  onSelectionsChange: (selections: Record<number, boolean>) => void;
  projectAssignments: Record<number, { mode: "new" | "existing"; projectId?: string; newName?: string }>;
  onProjectAssignmentsChange: (assignments: Record<number, { mode: "new" | "existing"; projectId?: string; newName?: string }>) => void;
  existingProjects: Project[];
}
```

The component renders the `<table>` with the "select all" checkbox header, the thead with Dependency/Version/Ecosystem/Source/Project columns, and the tbody rows. It uses `ecosystemColors` from the shared module. No state of its own -- fully controlled by parent.

#### Step 3: Create `web/components/dashboard/scan-url-tab.tsx`

New file. This is a simplified version of the onboard page's `OnboardPageInner`, adapted for inline dashboard use:

- **State machine**: `"input" | "scanning" | "results" | "applied"` (same as onboard page)
- **Input step**: A compact URL input with Scan button (same placeholder text as onboard page: "owner/repo or https://github.com/owner/repo"), plus helper text about supported files.
- **Scanning step**: Reuse the same spinner/progress UI from DepsTab (simpler than onboard page's ScanStep/StepConnector -- keeps visual weight lower in the dashboard card).
- **Results step**: Use the shared `ScanResultsTable` + a "Track Selected (N)" button and "Try another" back-link.
- **Applied step**: Success banner, skipped warnings, "Scan another repo" link. No "View Projects" button (since we are already on the dashboard).
- After successful apply: call `mutate("projects-for-dashboard")` and `mutate("projects-list")` (fix gap in current code).

Key differences from the standalone onboard page:
- No `useSearchParams` / `?repo=` auto-start (that stays in the onboard page only).
- No page-level header/title.
- Compact layout (same padding as StarsTab/DepsTab content area).

The core scan logic (call `onboard.scan()`, poll `onboard.getScan()`, call `onboard.apply()`) is unchanged.

#### Step 4: Modify `web/components/dashboard/suggestions-section.tsx`

Transform from a 2-tab container into a 3-tab container. Accept a new prop to control which tabs are visible:

```typescript
interface SuggestionsSectionProps {
  showAuthTabs?: boolean; // default false - when true, show Stars + Deps
}
```

Changes:
- Import `ScanUrlTab` alongside existing `StarsTab` and `DepsTab`.
- Tab type becomes `"scan" | "stars" | "deps"`.
- Default tab is `"scan"`.
- Render tabs conditionally: always render "Scan URL" tab button. Only render "Your Stars" and "Your Dependencies" tab buttons when `showAuthTabs` is true.
- Tab bar uses the same inline styling pattern already in place.
- Content area renders the active tab component.

The tab bar rendering (simplified):

```typescript
const tabs = [
  { key: "scan", label: "Scan URL" },
  ...(showAuthTabs ? [
    { key: "stars", label: "Your Stars" },
    { key: "deps", label: "Your Dependencies" },
  ] : []),
];
```

#### Step 5: Modify `web/components/dashboard/deps-tab.tsx`

- Remove the inline `ecosystemColors` declaration (lines 11-21).
- Import `ecosystemColors` from `./shared/ecosystem-colors`.
- Replace the inline results table JSX (lines 320-437) with `<ScanResultsTable>` component.
- Add `mutate("projects-for-dashboard")` and `mutate("projects-list")` to the `applySelections` success path (currently missing).

The DepsTab's unique behavior (repo picker step, repo list from `suggestions.repos()`) is untouched. Only the shared table rendering and the color constant are extracted.

#### Step 6: Modify `web/app/onboard/page.tsx`

- Remove the inline `ecosystemColors` declaration (lines 12-22).
- Import `ecosystemColors` from `@/components/dashboard/shared/ecosystem-colors`.
- Replace the inline results table JSX (lines 342-473) with `<ScanResultsTable>` component.
- The `ScanStep` and `StepConnector` sub-components stay in this file (they are only used here).
- The onboard page continues to work fully standalone (URL input, `?repo=` auto-start, "View Projects" button after apply). No behavioral changes.

#### Step 7: Modify `web/app/page.tsx`

Remove the Quick Onboard inline block (lines 80-136). Restructure the conditional rendering:

```tsx
{user?.github_login && user.github_login !== "dev" ? (
  <SuggestionsSection showAuthTabs />
) : (
  <>
    <DiscoverySection />
    <SuggestionsSection />
  </>
)}
```

Remove the `repoUrl` state, `router` (if no other use), and `Search` icon import that were only used by the Quick Onboard inline block.

The `router` is not used elsewhere in the current page.tsx (checked: only the Quick Onboard uses it). The `Search` import from `lucide-react` was only for the Quick Onboard button. Both can be removed along with the `useState` for `repoUrl`.

### 4. Resulting Layout

**Authenticated user dashboard:**
```
[Dashboard heading]
[Unified Card: Tab Bar = "Scan URL" | "Your Stars" | "Your Dependencies"]
  -> default tab: "Scan URL" (inline scan flow)
[Stats Cards]
[Release Trend | Unified Feed]
```

**Unauthenticated/dev user dashboard:**
```
[Dashboard heading]
[Trending Marquee (DiscoverySection)]
[Unified Card: Tab Bar = "Scan URL" only]
  -> "Scan URL" tab (inline scan flow)
[Stats Cards or Empty State]
```

### 5. Potential Challenges

1. **SWR key collision**: The ScanUrlTab and DepsTab both use `"projects-all"` SWR key for the project dropdown. This is fine -- SWR deduplicates identical keys, so both tabs share the same cache entry. No issue.

2. **Tab switching during in-progress scan**: If a user starts a scan in Scan URL tab, switches to Stars tab, then switches back, the scan polling effect should resume because the ScanUrlTab component re-mounts. The state (scanId, step) is local to ScanUrlTab, so it would be lost on unmount. Two approaches:
   - **Option A (simpler)**: Accept the reset. User sees the input step again. This matches current DepsTab behavior.
   - **Option B**: Lift scan state to SuggestionsSection. Over-engineered for little gain.
   
   **Recommendation**: Option A. Keep it simple. Add a note in the scanning UI: if the user navigates away and comes back, a re-scan of the same URL will return the existing active scan (the API returns 409 with the existing scan data).

3. **ScanResultsTable prop surface**: Keep it minimal. The table is purely presentational + controlled state. The apply button stays in the parent because the post-apply UX differs (DepsTab shows "Scan another repo" link, ScanUrlTab shows success + "Scan another", onboard page shows "View Projects" + "Scan Another Repo" buttons).

4. **Import path depth**: Using `./shared/ecosystem-colors` from dashboard components, `@/components/dashboard/shared/ecosystem-colors` from the onboard page. Standard Next.js path conventions, no issues.

### 6. Files Summary

| File | Action | Description |
|------|--------|-------------|
| `web/components/dashboard/shared/ecosystem-colors.ts` | **CREATE** | Shared `ecosystemColors` constant |
| `web/components/dashboard/shared/scan-results-table.tsx` | **CREATE** | Shared results table component |
| `web/components/dashboard/scan-url-tab.tsx` | **CREATE** | New "Scan URL" tab with inline scan flow |
| `web/components/dashboard/suggestions-section.tsx` | **MODIFY** | Add `showAuthTabs` prop, add "Scan URL" tab, import ScanUrlTab |
| `web/components/dashboard/deps-tab.tsx` | **MODIFY** | Replace inline table + colors with shared imports |
| `web/app/onboard/page.tsx` | **MODIFY** | Replace inline table + colors with shared imports |
| `web/app/page.tsx` | **MODIFY** | Remove Quick Onboard inline, restructure conditional rendering |

### Critical Files for Implementation
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/dashboard/suggestions-section.tsx` - Core container to transform into the unified 3-tab card
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/dashboard/deps-tab.tsx` - Reference pattern for scan flow and the results table to extract
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/onboard/page.tsx` - Second source of duplicated table/colors to refactor, must remain working standalone
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/page.tsx` - Dashboard page layout to restructure (remove Quick Onboard inline, wire up unified card)
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/client.ts` - API client methods (`onboard.scan`, `onboard.getScan`, `onboard.apply`) used by the new ScanUrlTab
